Selection reason: 为 NetOps agent 构建动作级安全信号并利用内部信号预测危害/进展，直接启发 agent 的预执行安全验证与 abstention harness。

TL;DR: 为 NetOps 智能体构建动作级真实基准并推导进展/危害标签；在 10 个模型上，利用内部信号的验证器比仅用可观测信号更可靠地预测危害与进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在自治网络控制回路中的有效性依赖长时程可靠性；在高风险数据中心场景中，避免高风险动作需预知动作影响，但现有基准缺少每动作真实标签。

Method: 对 NetArena 网络修复任务中的仿真网络进行符号重放，每一步与环境验证，得到每个动作精确价值；据此推导两个执行前目标：是否缩短修复距离（进展）和是否增加修复距离（危害）；比较智能体验证器的内部信号与仅可观测信号基线。

Result: 在 10 个智能体模型上，利用内部信号的智能体验证器预测危害和进展均比仅使用可观测信号的基线更可靠。

Conclusion: 这些信号有望作为智能体执行框架的安全反馈，使其避免高风险动作并保护目标系统。

中文摘要：智能体网络运维（NetOps）是一种新兴范式，有望实现工作负载感知、可自我调整且可靠的自治网络。尽管智能体已在事件摘要和遥测信号提取中证明了其价值，但它们作为自治控制回路引擎的有效性在很大程度上依赖于其长时程可靠性。其中一个场景是数据中心网络架构，智能体必须响应告警和运维人员意图，同时避免可能造成或延长停机的高风险动作。然而，弃权预设动作的影响在执行前已知，这需要每个动作的真实基准，而网络运维智能体基准并未提供这样的基准。我们为 NetArena 的网络修复任务构建了这样的真实基准。对仿真网络的符号重放每一步都与环境进行验证，从而得到每个动作的精确价值。基于动作级价值，我们推导出两个执行前目标，即动作是否缩短修复距离（进展）以及是否增加修复距离（危害）。我们在 10 个智能体模型上表明，利用内部信号的智能体验证器比仅使用可观测信号的基线更可靠地预测危害和进展。展望未来，我们旨在将这些信号用作智能体执行框架的安全反馈，以避免高风险动作并保护目标系统。

Original abstract: Agentic Network Operations (NetOps) are an emerging paradigm promising to enable workload-aware, self-adjustable, and reliable autonomous networks. While agents have proven their value in incident summarization and telemetry signal extraction, their effectiveness as autonomous control-loop engines heavily relies on their long-horizon reliability. One such setting is the datacenter fabric, where an agent must respond to alarms and operator intents while abstaining from high-risk actions that may cause or extend downtime. Abstention, however, presupposes that an action's impact is known pre-execution, which necessitates a per-action ground truth that NetOps agent benchmarks do not provide. We construct such a ground truth for the network repair task of NetArena. A symbolic replay of the emulated network, validated against the environment at every turn, yields the exact value of every action. From the action-level value, we derive two pre-execution targets, namely whether an action reduces the repair distance (progress) and whether it increases it (harm). We show across 10 agent models, that agent verifiers leveraging internal signals predict both harm and progress more reliably than a baseline using observable signals only. Perspectively, we aim to use these signals as safety feedback to an agent harness to abstain from risky actions and protect the target system.

</details>


### [31] [When does a scaling result justify a different allocation? A critical review of resource-allocation evidence for AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14500)
*Seyed Morteza Emadi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 35 pages, 3 figures, 9 tables

Selection reason: 讨论含检索、工具、验证与交互的AI系统在资源预算下的评估与分配决策，对agent评测和资源分配方法有方法学参考价值。

TL;DR: 该综述提出能力曲面与资源包络框架，用于判断扩展结果能否支持资源分配决策，并指出评估中的三类错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 扩展研究常以更大预算下更高分数作为依据，但这不足以说明资源应投向何处；需要明确报告结果在何种条件下支持资源分配决策。

Method: 比较预训练、测试时计算、检索与智能体评估证据，区分被测试流程性能与资源约束下最优性能；提出能力曲面和资源包络，并通过分析性示例与已发表比较进行说明。

Result: 识别出三类反复出现的评估错配：答案选定前计成功、使用部署中不可得信息、遗漏成本；说明评估指标、部署规模、选择规则和停止策略可改变分配结论；资源包络可记录结论所依赖的条件并揭示未决部署问题。

Conclusion: 该框架给出在可行系统间选择所需比较，并推动分配规则跨任务与运行条件的迁移实验；不提出普适扩展定律，也不从基准提升推断通用智能。

中文摘要：AI 扩展研究越来越多地评估将预训练模型与检索、搜索、验证、工具和交互相结合的系统。然而，在更大预算下获得更高分数本身并不能表明额外资源最好花在哪里。这篇批判性综合综述追问：一个报告出的扩展结果何时支持资源分配决策。它比较了预训练、测试时计算、检索和智能体评估中的证据，区分被测试过程的性能与在资源限制下可达到的最佳性能。该综合表明，这些证据中反复出现三种不匹配：在选定答案之前就计入成功、部署系统不会拥有的信息，以及比较中被遗漏的成本。能力曲面将性能表示为预算、机制和可用信息的函数。分析性示例展示了评估指标、部署规模、选择规则和停止策略如何改变分配结论。资源包络提供了一种结构化记录，涵盖任务、开发与运行时资源、信息访问以及报告分数背后的过程。将其应用于一项已发表的比较，说明了哪些结论得到证据支持，哪些部署问题仍未解决。由此得到的框架规定了在可行系统之间进行选择所需的比较，并推动关于分配规则跨任务和运行条件迁移的实验。它不提出普适的扩展定律，也不从基准提升推断通用智能。

Original abstract: AI scaling studies increasingly evaluate systems that combine a pretrained model with retrieval, search, verification, tools, and interaction. Yet a higher score under a larger budget does not by itself show where additional resources are best spent. This critical integrative review asks when a reported scaling result supports a resource-allocation decision. It compares evidence across pretraining, test-time computation, retrieval, and agent evaluation, distinguishing the performance of a tested procedure from the best performance achievable under a resource limit. The synthesis shows that three mismatches recur across this evidence: success counted before an answer is chosen, information a deployed system will not have, and costs left out of the comparison. A capability surface expresses performance as a function of budgets, mechanisms, and available information. Worked analytical examples show how the evaluation metric, deployment volume, selection rule, and stopping policy can alter an allocation conclusion. A resource envelope provides a structured record of the task, development and run-time resources, information access, and procedure behind a reported score. Its application to a published comparison illustrates which conclusions the evidence supports and which deployment questions remain unresolved. The resulting framework specifies the comparisons needed to choose among feasible systems and motivates experiments on the transfer of allocation rules across tasks and operating conditions. It does not propose a universal scaling law or infer general intelligence from benchmark gains.

</details>


### [32] [Beyond Scene Description: Multi-Agent Orchestration for Non-visual Access to Virtual Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14512)
*Toqeer Ali Syed,Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Danial Hameed*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Paper submitted in ICAAD Conference 2026

Selection reason: 提出八类专用agent加编排器的多agent架构，并基于相关性、置信度和负载评分筛选输出，可迁移到agent信息仲裁与harness设计。

TL;DR: MetaBlind 将虚拟世界中的非视觉访问拆分为八个专门代理，并由可访问性编排器按安全相关性、目标相关性、紧迫性、置信度、用户相关性和估计聆听负载筛选候选信息，仅通过语音、结构化音频或触觉输出呈现当时相关内容；目前仅为设计阶段，无原型测量或用户研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有盲人和视障辅助工具各自孤立解决命名物体、阅读文本、描述场景或规划路线等单一任务，而实时虚拟房间中障碍物、说话者、手势、聊天、幻灯片和通知并发出现，直接全部叙述会把视觉障碍转化为听觉过载，因此需要能统一协调非视觉信息呈现的架构。

Method: 提出 MetaBlind 架构：用八个专门代理覆盖感知、导航、社交与对象交互、通信、安全与信任、记忆和个性化；代理不直接对用户说话，而是把候选信息发布到共享可访问性上下文；可访问性编排器对候选项按安全相关性、目标相关性、紧迫性、置信度、用户相关性和估计聆听负载评分，并仅释放当时相关的项目；形式化选择步骤，将编排循环表示为算法，并定义针对单代理助手的评估协议。

Result: MetaBlind 处于设计阶段报告，没有原型测量或用户研究；论文给出评估协议，并说明哪些结果会支持该设计、哪些结果会反驳该设计。

Conclusion: MetaBlind 通过多代理与可访问性编排器缓解实时虚拟环境中的信息过载，将非视觉访问从单任务工具推进到统一编排框架；其有效性尚待按所定义协议进行原型与用户研究验证。

中文摘要：虚拟世界如今承载着教室、会议、大会、商店和社交场所，而它们所暴露的几乎每一种交互都假设用户能够扫描三维场景、跟随化身并阅读浮动面板。盲人和视障（BVI）用户只能使用各自孤立解决一项任务的辅助工具：命名一个物体、阅读文本、描述一个场景或规划一条路线。一个实时虚拟房间会使这种模型失效：障碍物、说话者、手势、聊天、幻灯片和通知会同时到达，而一个将其全部叙述出来的工具只是把视觉障碍换成了听觉障碍。本文提出 MetaBlind，一种将非视觉访问分布到八个专门代理的架构，这些代理涵盖感知、导航、社交与对象交互、通信、安全与信任、记忆和个性化，并在这些代理与用户之间放置一个可访问性编排器。代理将候选信息发布到共享的可访问性上下文中，而不是直接对用户说话。编排器根据安全相关性、目标相关性、紧迫性、置信度、用户相关性和估计聆听负载对每个候选项评分，然后仅通过语音、结构化音频或触觉输出释放它判断当时相关的项目。我们给出选择步骤的形式化陈述，将编排循环规定为一种算法，并定义针对单代理助手的评估协议。MetaBlind 处于设计阶段报告，没有原型测量或用户研究，并且该协议说明哪些结果会支持该设计、哪些会反驳它。

Original abstract: Virtual worlds now host classrooms, meetings, conferences, shops, and social venues, and nearly every interaction they expose assumes a user who can scan a three-dimensional scene, follow avatars, and read floating panels. Blind and visually impaired (BVI) users are left with assistive tools that each solve one task in isolation: naming an object, reading text, describing a scene, or planning a route. A live virtual room defeats that model: obstacles, speakers, gestures, chat, slides, and notifications arrive together, and a tool that narrates all of them trades a visual barrier for an auditory one. This paper presents MetaBlind, an architecture that distributes nonvisual access across eight specialized agents, spanning perception, navigation, social and object interaction, communication, safety and trust, memory, and personalization, and that places an Accessibility Orchestrator between those agents and the user. Agents publish candidate information into a shared accessibility context instead of speaking to the user directly. The orchestrator scores each candidate on safety relevance, goal relevance, urgency, confidence, user relevance, and estimated listening load, then releases only the items it judges relevant at that moment through speech, structured audio, or haptic output. We give the selection step a formal statement, specify the orchestration cycle as an algorithm, and define an evaluation protocol against a single-agent assistant. MetaBlind is reported at the design stage, with no prototype measurement or user study, and the protocol states which outcomes would support the design and which would refute it.

</details>


### [33] [AI Deployment Accountability Engineering: A Vision for Accountable AI in Safety-Critical Socio-Technical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14592)
*Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Appeared in ACM AI Summit 26, Atlanta, GA as a vision paper

Selection reason: 提出部署层可问责AI工程议程，关注agentic AI的系统级风险、失败归因与及时干预，对agent可靠性控制有参考意义。

TL;DR: 提出ADAE子学科，将问责从模型层扩展到部署层，并围绕四大支柱构建研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估以模型为中心，部署前属性如准确性、鲁棒性、公平性、可解释性虽必要，但在动态社会技术环境中运行后并不充分。

Method: 作为愿景论文，提出AI部署问责工程（ADAE），将问责定义为部署层属性，并设计四个相互关联的研究支柱。

Result: 研究议程包括情境相关故障模式发现、隐私保护问责测量、面向智能体式AI的系统级风险分析，以及技术故障向运营和制度风险的转化。

Conclusion: 目标是建立可问责AI部署的基础原则、数学工具和系统架构，以服务安全关键应用。

中文摘要：人工智能系统正迅速成为医疗保健、金融、公共服务及其他安全关键领域的关键组成部分。然而，用于评估这些系统的工程实践仍以模型为中心，侧重于部署前的准确性、鲁棒性、公平性和可解释性等属性。一旦人工智能系统在由分布偏移、制度约束、人类反馈循环、隐私要求以及多个AI智能体之间的交互所刻画且不断变化的社会技术环境中运行，这些属性虽是必要的，却并不充分。

这篇愿景论文提出人工智能部署问责工程（ADAE），一个拟议的人工智能工程子学科，关注为已部署的人工智能系统建立可测量、持续且可操作的问责。ADAE将问责视为部署层属性，而非仅仅是单个模型的属性。它旨在确定人工智能赋能系统是否仍在可接受的风险限度内运行，识别故障出现的语境，将故障归因于相互交织的技术和人类组件，将技术故障转化为下游后果，并支持及时干预。

我们围绕四个相互关联的支柱提出研究议程：情境相关故障模式的结构化发现、隐私保护问责测量、面向智能体式人工智能的系统级风险分析，以及将技术故障转化为运营和制度风险。更广泛的目标是为安全关键应用中可问责的人工智能部署建立基础原则、数学工具和系统架构。

Original abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are rapidly becoming critical components in healthcare, finance, public services, and other safety-critical domains. Yet the engineering practices used to evaluate these systems remain predominantly model-centric, emphasizing properties such as accuracy, robustness, fairness, and interpretability before deployment. These properties are necessary but insufficient once an AI system operates within an ever changing socio-technical environment characterized by distribution shifts, institutional constraints, human feedback loops, privacy requirements, and interactions among multiple AI agents.
  This vision paper introduces AI Deployment Accountability Engineering (ADAE), a proposed AI engineering subdiscipline concerned with establishing measurable, continuous, and actionable accountability for deployed AI systems. ADAE treats accountability as a deployment-layer property rather than solely as a property of an individual model. It seeks to determine whether an AI-enabled system continues to operate within acceptable risk limits, identify the contexts in which failures emerge, attribute failures across interacting technical and human components, translate technical failures into downstream consequences, and support timely intervention.
  We articulate a research agenda built around four interconnected pillars: structured discovery of context-dependent failure modes, privacy-preserving accountability measurement, system-level risk analysis for agentic AI, and translation of technical failures into operational, and institutional risks. The broader goal is to establish foundational principles, mathematical tools, and system architectures for accountable AI deployment across safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [34] [A note on goal-based hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14605)
*Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用层次隐马尔可夫模型统一目标选择、终止判断与内部信念状态，可作为agent规划与长程决策的形式化启发。

TL;DR: 本文用分层隐马尔可夫模型统一并扩展了以智能体为中心的通用价值函数构造与基于内部信念状态和内部目标的通用智能体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACGVF 框架允许智能体自主选择目标和目标终止条件，非常通用，但假设环境完全可观测；另一通用智能体设计引入了内部信念状态和内部目标，却假定目标由外部提供。因此需要统一并扩展这两种方法。

Method: 使用分层隐马尔可夫模型（HHMM）的形式体系来统一并扩展 ACGVF 构造与基于内部信念状态和内部目标的通用智能体设计。

Result: 本文在 HHMM 形式体系下统一并扩展了上述两种方法；摘要未报告实验或定量结果。

Conclusion: 本文表明，通过 HHMM 可以统一并扩展 ACGVF 构造与基于内部信念和内部目标的通用智能体设计。

中文摘要：\citet{tasse2026goal} 的以智能体为中心的通用价值函数（ACGVF）构造允许智能体做出两项通常由环境或智能体设计者强加的决定：追求哪个目标以及何时宣布目标完成（除了选择动作之外）。这是一个非常通用的框架，几乎涵盖了先前关于强化学习、控制和规划的所有工作，以及认知科学中提出的更一般的形式体系。然而，它假设环境是完全可观测的，即观测是充分统计量。在 \citet{murphy2025rl} 中，提出了一种通用智能体设计，其中策略基于内部信念状态 $z_t$ 和内部目标；然而，目标被假定为外部提供。在本注记中，我们使用分层隐马尔可夫模型（HHMM）\citep{murphy2001hhmm} 的形式体系来统一并扩展这两种方法。

Original abstract: The agent-centric general value function (ACGVF) construction of
  \citet{tasse2026goal} lets the agent make two decisions that are
  normally imposed by the environment or agent designer: which goal to
  pursue and when to declare a goal as finished (in addition to
  choosing the action). This is a very general framework that
  subsumes almost all prior work on reinforcement learning, control
  and planning, as well as more general formalisms proposed in the
  cognitive sciences. However, it assumes the environment is fully
  observed, i.e., that the observation is a sufficient statistic. In
  \citet{murphy2025rl}, a general agent design was proposed where the
  policy is based on an internal belief state $z_t$ and an internal
  goal; however, the goals were assumed to be externally provided. In
  this note, we unify and extend these two approaches using the
  formalism of hierarchical hidden Markov models (HHMM) \citep{murphy2001hhmm}.

</details>


### [35] [DynSTEER: Dynamic Stage-wise Trajectory Evaluation and Execution-time Review for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14637)
*Zhichao Shi,Xuhui Jiang,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Chengjin Xu,Jian Guo,Yuanzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 直接研究长程agent轨迹的分阶段动态评估、多级审查与失败早停，与自动代码优化agent的轨迹评测高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出DynSTEER动态分阶段轨迹评估框架，弥合全轨迹与原子步骤之间的粒度差距，提升判别性并节省失败执行步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体轨迹评估存在粒度困境：全轨迹验证太粗，原子步骤评分太细且噪声敏感、计算昂贵；单参考轨迹范式无法覆盖有效执行路径的多样性，并延迟反馈与提前停止。

Method: 提出DynSTEER：在关键执行节点分段rollout并进行阶段级动态评估，按阶段结果自适应多层审查；从公开任务视图构建路径容忍里程碑图，保留合法路径且不泄漏参考；支持终止不可恢复执行以减少资源浪费。

Result: 评估判别能力较原生评估提升85.2%，在所有模型对间实现统计显著区分，并在失败rollouts上节省45.41%执行步骤。

Conclusion: DynSTEER能够弥合轨迹评估的粒度差距，提高评估判别性与效率，并减少失败长时程执行的资源浪费。

中文摘要：大语言模型智能体正越来越多地被部署用于长时程任务执行，这引发了轨迹评估中的一个核心粒度问题：全轨迹验证过于粗糙，无法捕捉长轨迹中的具体失败及其相关证据；而原子步骤评分则过于细粒度、对噪声敏感且计算成本高昂。这种粒度差距使得单参考轨迹范式不足以评估有效智能体执行路径的丰富空间，并延迟了长时程任务中的及时反馈和提前停止。为解决这些问题，我们提出了DynSTEER，一种用于智能体轨迹评估的动态分阶段框架。DynSTEER通过分阶段动态评估弥合粒度差距，该评估在关键执行节点处对rollouts进行分段，并根据阶段级结果调整其多层审查策略；它从公开任务视图中编译出一个路径容忍的里程碑图，以在无参考泄漏的情况下保留多样的合法路径；并且它支持终止不可恢复的智能体执行，以抑制资源浪费。实验表明，DynSTEER将评估判别能力较原生评估提升了85.2%，以统计显著性区分了所有模型对，并在失败rollouts上节省了45.41%的执行步骤。

Original abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed for long-horizon task execution, raising a central granularity question for trajectory evaluation: whole-trajectory verification is too coarse to capture concrete failures and their associated evidence in long trajectories, while atomic-step scoring is too fine-grained, noise-sensitive, and computationally expensive. This granularity gap makes a single-reference trajectory paradigm inadequate for assessing the rich space of valid agent execution paths and delays timely feedback and early stopping in long-horizon tasks. To address these issues, we propose DynSTEER, a dynamic stage-wise framework for agent trajectory evaluation. DynSTEER bridges the granularity gap through stage-wise dynamic evaluation that segments rollouts at key execution nodes and adapts its multi-tier review strategy based on stage-level results; it compiles a path-tolerant milestone graph from public task views to preserve diverse legal paths without reference leakage; and it supports terminating unrecoverable agent executions to curb resource waste. Experiments show that DynSTEER improves evaluation discriminability by 85.2\% over native evaluation, separates all model pairs with statistical significance, and saves 45.41\% of execution steps on failed rollouts.

</details>


### [36] [Moral Rebel Agents: Decision-Making Under Conflicting Obligations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14716)
*Hector Munoz-Avila,David W. Aha,Paola Rizzo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 1 table, 3 figures

Journal reference: Hector Munoz-Avila, David W. Aha, and Paola Rizzo, "Moral Rebel Agents: Decision-Making Under Conflicting Obligations," Proceedings of the Thirteenth Annual Conference on Advances in Cognitive Systems, 2026

Selection reason: 形式化多种自主agent在冲突义务下的决策架构并用HTN规划评估，可迁移到agent规划、承诺保持与安全约束设计。

TL;DR: 论文研究自主智能体在道德正当时偏离用户分配任务的道德反叛能力，形式化五类智能体架构，在分层任务网络规划与迷你搜索救援域中评估，发现任务承诺保持是关键维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体通常被要求服从用户分配的任务，但严格服从可能与执行中产生的道德义务冲突，因此需要研究道德反叛。

Method: 形式化五类架构：非道德智能体，以及功利主义、道义论、功利-道义（UD）和尽职四种道德反叛形式；在分层任务网络规划框架中实现，并在迷你搜索与救援域中评估。

Result: 不同智能体在救援结果、分配任务完成和规范合规之间呈现不同权衡；UD与尽职智能体虽共享功利主义和道义论基础，却因任务承诺保持而产生显著不同行为。

Conclusion: 承诺感知的道德推理对在道德攸关环境中运行的自主智能体十分重要。

中文摘要：自主智能体通常有义务遵循用户分配的任务。然而，严格服从可能与执行过程中出现的道德义务发生冲突。本文研究道德反叛（moral rebellion）：自主智能体在道德上正当时偏离用户分配任务的能力。我们形式化了五种智能体架构：一种非道德智能体（amoral agent），其不考虑道德义务而追求分配任务；以及四种道德反叛能动性形式：（1）功利主义智能体，机会主义地最大化任务结果；（2）道义论智能体，执行规范性约束；（3）功利-道义（UD）智能体，将道义约束与功利推理相结合；（4）尽职智能体，此外还保持对分配任务的承诺。我们在分层任务网络规划框架中实现这些架构，并在一个迷你搜索与救援域中评估它们，该域暴露了分配任务完成、机会主义救援和规范合规之间的权衡。我们的实证结果表明，所提出的智能体在救援结果、分配任务完成和规范合规之间表现出不同的权衡。特别是，任务承诺的保持成为道德反叛的一个重要维度：尽管UD智能体和尽职智能体共享功利主义和道义论基础，但它们产生显著不同的行为。这些发现凸显了在具有道德后果的环境中运行的自主智能体进行承诺感知道德推理的重要性。

Original abstract: Autonomous agents are typically obliged to follow user-assigned tasks. However, strict obedience may conflict with moral obligations that arise during execution. This paper investigates \textbf{moral rebellion}: the ability of an autonomous agent to deviate from a user-assigned task when morally justified. We formalize five agent architectures: an \textit{amoral agent} that pursues assigned tasks without considering moral obligations, and four forms of \textbf{moral rebel agency}: (1) \textit{utilitarian agents} that opportunistically maximize task outcomes; (2) \textit{deontic} agents that enforce normative constraints; (3) \textit{utilitarian-deontic} (UD) agents that combine deontic constraints with utilitarian reasoning; and (4) \textit{dutiful} agents that additionally preserve commitments to assigned tasks. We implement these architectures within a hierarchical task network planning framework and evaluate them in a Mini Search-and-Rescue domain that exposes trade-offs among assigned-task completion, opportunistic rescue, and norm compliance. Our empirical results show that the proposed agents exhibit distinct trade-offs among rescue results, assigned-task completion, and norm compliance. In particular, the preservation of task commitments emerges as an important dimension of moral rebellion, for which the UD and dutiful agents produce substantially different behaviors despite their shared utilitarian and deontological foundations. These findings highlight the importance of commitment-aware moral reasoning for autonomous agents operating in morally consequential environments.

</details>


### [37] [AppliedScientist: Automated Scientific Revision Through Iterative AI Reviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14738)
*Vidushee Vats,Karun Sharma,Shengzhi Li,Shichao Pei*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 构建AI科学家与AI评审闭环，通过迭代修订和自反思改进论文，对agent自我改进、反思与迭代工作流有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出闭环系统 AppliedScientist，将 AI 科学家与 AI 评审者结合，通过迭代修改被拒论文检验评审引导修改的有效性；评审引导修改优于固定提示自我修改，且更能解决执行类而非想法类弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化评审多按评审文本质量评估，但评审价值应体现在依据其修改能否带来论文可衡量改进；因此需要一个闭环、可迭代修改论文的评估框架。

Method: 构建 AppliedScientist，将自主 AI 科学家与 AI 评审者耦合；AI 科学家修改时可访问先前版本，而评审者每次独立生成、无先前反馈或评分记忆。比较三种修改设置：原始会议/期刊评审初始化、AI 评审初始化、每轮相同固定提示的自主自我修改。另用 Stanford Reviewer 作为独立评估者评估人类评审初始化的修改。

Result: 评审者引导的修改持续优于固定提示自我修改，Stanford Reviewer 对后续修改版本评分更高。AppliedScientist 解决 150 个执行相关弱点中的 128 个（85.3%），但仅解决 18 个想法相关弱点中的 2 个（11.1%）。

Conclusion: 迭代修改能有效改善实验与实现，却很少改变对新颖性或重要性的担忧。

中文摘要：自动化评审系统正越来越多地根据其生成评审的质量来评估。然而，只有当依据评审采取行动能使论文产生可衡量的改进时，评审才是有用的。我们提出 AppliedScientist，一个将自主 AI 科学家与 AI 评审者耦合起来的闭环系统，并通过迭代修改来自多个研究子领域的被拒论文来评估它。为了模拟人类作者如何在早期草稿基础上改进，AI 科学家在修改过程中可以访问其先前版本。然而，为了避免先前判断带来的偏差，每次评审都独立生成，评审者不记得先前的反馈或评分。我们比较三种修改设置：一种用原始会议/期刊评审初始化，一种用 AI 生成的评审初始化，以及每轮使用相同固定提示的自主自我修改。由于评审者既指导修改又评估修改，我们还使用 Stanford Reviewer 作为独立评估者来评估以人类评审初始化的修改。由评审者引导的修改始终比固定提示的自我修改改进更多，Stanford Reviewer 也给较晚的修改版本分配更高的分数。AppliedScientist 解决了 150 个与执行相关的弱点中的 128 个（85.3%），但只解决了 18 个与想法相关的弱点中的 2 个（11.1%），这表明迭代修改能有效改进实验和实现，但很少改变对新颖性或重要性的担忧。

Original abstract: Automated reviewing systems are increasingly evaluated based on the quality of the reviews they produce. Yet a review is only useful if acting on it leads to a measurable improvement in the paper. We present AppliedScientist, a closed-loop system that couples an autonomous AI scientist with an AI reviewer, and evaluate it by iteratively revising rejected papers from a range of research subfields. To mirror how human authors build on earlier drafts, the AI scientist has access to its previous versions during revision. To avoid bias from prior judgments, however, each review is generated independently, with the reviewer having no memory of earlier feedback or scores. We compare three revision settings: one initialized with the original venue reviews, one initialized with AI-generated reviews, and autonomous self-revision using the same fixed prompt in every round. Because the reviewer both guides and evaluates the revision, we also assess the human-initialized revisions using Stanford Reviewer as an independent evaluator. Reviewer-guided revision consistently improves more than fixed-prompt self-revision, and Stanford Reviewer also assigns higher scores to later revisions. AppliedScientist resolves 128 of 150 execution-related weaknesses (85.3%), but only 2 of 18 idea-related weaknesses (11.1%), suggesting that iterative revision is effective at improving experiments and implementation, but rarely changes concerns about novelty or significance.

</details>


### [38] [AcquireBound: Runtime Authorization for Resources Acquired by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14744)
*Genliang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 54 pages, 1 figure, 9 tables, 4 algorithms. Preprint

Selection reason: 针对AI agent获取资源后的运行时授权与能力边界控制，提出隔离、解析和激活机制，直接关联agent安全与工具使用控制。

TL;DR: AcquireBound 通过隔离获取结果、基于认证证据解析能力，并在激活事务中用来源与向下封闭关系包络校验，弥补履约后激活缺口；证明八项安全属性，并在轨迹、篡改、MCP、Docker 组合与审计实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 AI 代理可通过获取算力、凭据、账户、服务和代理等引入新权限，而现有支付、预算、OAuth、授权与履约检查只验证交易条件，无法判断返回资源是否会转化为可用权限，形成履约后激活缺口。

Method: 提出 AcquireBound：隔离获取输出，经版本化解析器从认证提供方证据解析实际能力，并通过当前激活事务校验清单、来源、纪元和类型化资源-能力超图上的向下封闭关系包络；一次性效果许可在效果线性化时重新验证并消耗；在明确假设下证明安全属性。

Result: 五类资源、810 事件上参考语义接受 20/20 良性轨迹、拒绝 40/40 已注册不安全轨迹；独立检查器与 60 基础/40 细化轨迹一致，拒绝 89/89 篡改测试；Codex/Gemini MCP 客户端完成 54/54 本地 stdio 调用；18 案例 MCP-to-Docker 组合中良性路径全通过，16 条不安全路径均未新增未授权 Docker 启动请求；五源审计分类 32 单元 1,248 字段对，无单一单元提供完整激活配置。

Conclusion: AcquireBound 表明履约后激活需被作为独立授权边界处理，来源受限的能力解析与激活事务可在多种资源、工具调用和组合场景中提供安全保证。

中文摘要：通过获取算力、凭据、账户、服务和其他代理，自主 AI 代理可以将新权限引入任务。支付、预算、OAuth、授权和履约检查可以验证交易条件，却无法判定返回的资源是否可能成为可用权限。这种履约后激活缺口横跨工具介导的创建、代理间委派和代理式商务。我们提出 AcquireBound，一种来源受限的运行时授权架构。它隔离获取的输出，通过版本化解析器从经认证的提供方证据中解析其实际能力，并且仅通过当前激活事务来激活它们，该事务检查已解析清单、来源、纪元以及类型化资源-能力超图上的向下封闭关系包络。该包络保持关联的身份、效果、数据、委派和图范围限制。一次性效果许可在效果线性化时被重新验证并消耗。在明确假设下，我们证明八个安全属性，涵盖隔离、背书、非放大、拆分不可规避、崩溃/重试、退款、纪元以及效果限制。在五类资源上，参考语义在 810 个事件上接受了 20/20 条良性轨迹，并拒绝了 40/40 条已注册不安全轨迹；独立检查器在 60 条基础轨迹和 40 条细化轨迹上结果一致，并拒绝了 89/89 个篡改测试。冻结的 Codex 和 Gemini 模型上下文协议（MCP）客户端组件完成了 54/54 次确定性本地 stdio 调用。在注册的 18 案例分阶段 MCP-to-Docker 组合中，两条良性路径均完成，且 16 条不安全路径均未添加未经授权的 Docker 启动请求。一项五源审计对 32 个单元中的 1,248 个字段对进行了分类；没有任何单一单元提供完整的激活配置文件。

Original abstract: By acquiring compute, credentials, accounts, services, and other agents, autonomous AI agents can introduce new authority into a task. Payment, budget, OAuth, mandate, and fulfillment checks can validate transaction conditions without deciding whether a returned resource may become usable authority. This post-fulfillment activation gap spans tool-mediated creation, inter-agent delegation, and agentic commerce. We present AcquireBound, a provenance-bounded runtime authorization architecture. It quarantines acquired outputs, resolves their actual capabilities from authenticated provider evidence through a versioned resolver, and activates them only through a current activation transaction that checks the resolved manifest, provenance, epochs, and a downward-closed relational envelope over a typed resource-capability hypergraph. The envelope preserves correlated identity, effect, data, delegation, and graph-wide limits. Single-use effect permits are revalidated and consumed at effect linearization. Under explicit assumptions, we prove eight safety properties covering quarantine, backing, non-amplification, split non-evasion, crash/retry, refunds, epochs, and effect confinement. Across five resource classes, reference semantics accepted 20/20 benign traces and rejected 40/40 registered unsafe traces over 810 events; an independent checker agreed on 60 base and 40 refinement traces and rejected 89/89 tamper tests. Frozen Codex and Gemini Model Context Protocol (MCP) client components completed 54/54 deterministic local stdio calls. In a registered 18-case staged MCP-to-Docker composition, both benign paths completed, and none of the 16 unsafe paths added an unauthorized Docker start request. A five-source audit classified 1,248 field pairs across 32 units; no unit alone supplied a complete activation profile.

</details>


### [39] [ANASSA: An Agentic AI Orchestration Framework for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14824)
*Constantinos Papantoniou,Brian Hilton*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出agentic GIS编排框架，包含多agent工作流、执行反馈、验证与溯源治理，虽有垂直背景但编排设计可迁移到agent harness。

TL;DR: 本文综述智能体GIS进展，指出推理、执行与评估集成不足，并提出架构级框架ANASSA，集成空间推理、多智能体编排、验证、溯源与治理，暂未进行实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM/LMM推动GIS向半自主智能体工作流转变，但现有方案在复杂真实环境中存在推理、执行、评估碎片化，且空间推理、鲁棒性、验证、治理与评估仍有局限。

Method: 综合智能体GIS框架、基准和综述的最新进展，识别局限；提出ANASSA统一架构，包含结构化空间推理、多智能体工作流编排、执行反馈、权威空间验证、溯源、不确定性处理与人类决策权，并给出四层十一组件、六步认知循环、跨组件契约和治理机制。

Result: 提出ANASSA架构级规范：四层十一组件、六步Geospatial AI认知循环、跨组件契约与治理机制，旨在实现可追溯、可复现、可问责的智能体地理空间工作流；未报告实证性能评估。

Conclusion: 该研究将贡献限定为架构设计，实证性能评估需通过实现与部署研究进一步验证。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）与大型多模态模型（LMMs）的出现，催生了一类新型智能体系统，能够将自然语言理解与基于工具的执行相结合。在地理信息系统（GIS）中，这一转变正在把传统的、专家驱动的工作流转变为半自主系统，此类系统可以解释用户意图、构建空间工作流并执行地理空间分析任务。然而，现有方法仍受限于推理、执行与评估的碎片化集成，尤其在复杂的真实世界环境中。本研究综合了智能体GIS框架、基准与综述的最新进展，以识别空间推理、执行鲁棒性、验证、治理与评估方面的局限。基于这些洞见，研究提出了ANASSA（Autonomous Neural Agents for Spatial Systems Architecture），一个智能体AI编排框架，它在统一的系统设计中集成了结构化空间推理、多智能体工作流编排、执行反馈、权威空间验证、来源追溯、不确定性处理与人类决策权。其贡献是一项架构级规范：四层中的十一个组件、一个六步地理空间AI认知循环、跨组件契约，以及旨在使智能体地理空间工作流可追溯、可复现和可问责的治理机制。实证性能评估留待实现与部署研究进行。

Original abstract: The emergence of large language models (LLMs) and large multimodal models (LMMs) has enabled a new class of agentic systems capable of integrating natural language understanding with tool-based execution. In geographic information systems (GIS), this shift is transforming traditional, expert-driven workflows into semiautonomous systems that can interpret user intent, construct spatial workflows, and execute geospatial analysis tasks. However, existing approaches remain limited by fragmented integration of reasoning, execution, and evaluation, particularly in complex, real-world environments. This study synthesizes recent advances in agentic GIS frameworks, benchmarks, and surveys to identify limitations in spatial reasoning, execution robustness, validation, governance, and evaluation. Building on these insights, it introduces ANASSA (Autonomous Neural Agents for Spatial Systems Architecture), an agentic AI orchestration framework that integrates structured spatial reasoning, multi-agent workflow orchestration, execution feedback, authoritative spatial validation, provenance, uncertainty handling, and human decision authority within a unified system design. The contribution is an architecture-level specification: eleven components across four layers, a six-step Geospatial AI Cognitive Loop, cross-component contracts, and governance mechanisms intended to make agentic geospatial workflows traceable, reproducible, and accountable. Empirical performance evaluation is reserved for implementation and deployment studies.

</details>


### [40] [El Agente Potente: High-Throughput Agentic Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14840)
*Tsz Wai Ko,Jiaru Bai,Thomas Swanick,Yeonghun Kang,Changhyeok Choi,Angelina Qihong Jiang,Aiwei Yin,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 64 pages, 16 figures, and 3 tables, including Supporting Information. Main text: 24 pages, 6 figures, and 1 table

Selection reason: 结合类型化执行图与编码agent构建原子模拟工作流，LLM负责规划路由、确定性代码负责计算验证，对agent工作流与代码生成优化有直接启发。

TL;DR: El Agente Potente 是面向 MLIPs 原子模拟的智能体系统，用类型化执行图保障标准工作流的结构化、可溯源执行，并用编码智能体支持定制工作流；在材料发现、分子能量景观、吸附和催化等任务中验证，并评估可重复性与 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础 MLIPs 能以远低于从头算的成本在大型化学空间实现近从头算精度，但高通量性质计算中，如何将高层科学意图转化为自适应模拟活动且不牺牲工作流严谨性仍是核心挑战。

Method: 提出 El Agente Potente，结合类型化执行图与编码模式：类型化执行图用于标准化工作流，LLM 仅负责规划与路由，确定性 Python 组件执行计算与验证；编码智能体在需要更大流程灵活性时构建定制工作流，并调用现有 Potente 函数。

Result: 在计算材料发现、分子能量景观探索、吸附和催化反应工作流中演示 El Agente Potente，并开展可重复性和 LLM token 成本的系统性基准测试。

Conclusion: 结果表明，类型化执行图和基于代码的工作流构建是智能体科学计算的互补机制，可兼顾受控、可审计执行与定制原子模拟所需灵活性。

中文摘要：基础性机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）正以计算成本的一小部分，在大型化学空间中达到接近从头算的精度，从而改变原子模拟。将这些工具用于高通量性质计算的一个核心挑战是，在不损害工作流严谨性的情况下，将高层科学意图转化为自适应模拟活动。我们提出 El Agente Potente，一个将类型化执行图与互补的编码模式相结合、用于 MLIPs 驱动原子模拟的智能体系统。类型化执行图为标准化工作流提供结构化且溯源感知的执行，其中大语言模型（LLMs）仅限于规划和路由，而确定性 Python 组件执行科学计算和验证。作为对这种结构化执行的补充，编码智能体为需要更大流程灵活性的任务构建定制工作流，同时为受支持的计算调用现有 Potente 函数。我们在计算材料发现、分子能量景观探索、吸附和催化反应工作流中展示了 El Agente Potente，并给出了可重复性和 LLM token 成本的系统性基准测试。这些结果确立了类型化执行图和基于代码的工作流构建作为智能体科学计算的互补机制，将受控、可审计的执行与定制原子模拟所需的灵活性相结合。

Original abstract: Foundational machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are transforming atomistic simulations by achieving near-ab initio accuracy across large chemical spaces at a fraction of the computational cost. A central challenge in using these tools for high-throughput property calculations is translating high-level scientific intent into adaptive simulation campaigns without compromising workflow rigour. We introduce El Agente Potente, an agentic system that combines typed execution graphs with a complementary coding mode for MLIPs-driven atomistic simulations. Typed execution graphs provide structured and provenance-aware execution for standardized workflows, with large language models (LLMs) restricted to planning and routing while deterministic Python components perform scientific computation and validation. Complementing this structured execution, a coding agent constructs customized workflows for tasks requiring greater procedural flexibility while invoking existing Potente functions for supported calculations. We demonstrate El Agente Potente across computational materials discovery, molecular energy-landscape exploration, adsorption, and catalytic reaction workflows, together with systematic benchmarks of reproducibility and LLM token cost. These results establish typed execution graphs and code-based workflow construction as complementary mechanisms for agentic scientific computing, combining controlled, auditable execution with the flexibility required for customized atomistic simulations

</details>


### [41] [MemRiskBench: Trace-Aware Risk-Preserving Evaluation for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14976)
*Jianhua Jiang,Dongbo Yuan,Weihua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 11 pages, 4 figures,

Selection reason: 针对长程 LLM agent 记忆风险的五类分类法与轨迹级评测，含风险保持子集选择器，直接关联 agent 评测与可靠性。

TL;DR: MemRiskBench 提出五类长期记忆风险分类、无需 LLM 评判的确定性轨迹检查，以及能保持排序、风险覆盖与高风险检测的子集选择器，用于评估长时程 LLM 智能体的稀疏高风险故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM 智能体跨会话积累记忆，带来稀疏但高影响的风险；标准聚合分数和基准压缩会掩盖各风险失败率及罕见高严重性事件。

Method: 构建 120 回合脚本化基准，采用五类风险分类（外加一个已记录但不计分类别）和确定性轨迹接地检查，在通过/失败路径上不使用 LLM-as-judge，评估五个本地运行的量化指令微调模型；并提出基于确定性轨迹导出特征的覆盖约束贪心子集选择器。

Result: 在 20% 子集规模下，选择器保持完整排序（Spearman rho = 0.975，确定性，CI 为零 bootstrap 方差点估计）、风险覆盖 1.0、高风险模型检测 1.0，并将计算量减少 5 倍；基准还表明平均准确率 78% 的模型仍可能在 4% 回合中泄漏数据。

Conclusion: MemRiskBench 支持对已部署 LLM 智能体进行可复现评估和风险评估，并发布所有回合、轨迹、评分实现和选择器，强调按风险类型与严重性而非仅聚合准确率进行评估。

中文摘要：长时程 LLM 智能体跨会话积累记忆，产生稀疏但高影响的风险：过时事实、冲突更新、跨用户泄漏、已撤销记忆的复用以及约束衰减。标准聚合分数掩盖了各风险类型的失败率——一个达到 78% 平均准确率的模型仍可能在 4% 的回合中泄漏数据——而基准压缩会优先丢弃罕见的高严重性事件，而这些事件正是区分一个大体可用的模型与一个偶尔造成伤害的模型的关键。我们提出 MemRiskBench。主要贡献是一个五类风险分类法（外加一个已记录但不计分的类别），通过确定性的、以轨迹为依据的检查进行操作化，实例化为一个包含 120 个回合的脚本化基准，具有完整轨迹日志，并且在通过/失败路径上不使用 LLM-as-judge，在五个本地运行的量化指令微调模型上进行了评估。其次，一个风险保持的子集选择器：一种基于确定性轨迹导出特征的覆盖约束贪心选择器，其在 20% 子集规模下保持完整排序（Spearman rho = 0.975，确定性；CI 坍缩为零 bootstrap 方差的点估计）、风险覆盖（1.0）和高风险模型检测（1.0），并将计算量减少 5 倍。与仅关注排序的子集选择器不同，该选择器还使用无需 LLM 评判者的、以轨迹为依据的确定性特征，保持风险类型覆盖和高风险检测。所有回合、轨迹、评分实现和选择器均已发布，以支持对已部署 LLM 智能体的可复现评估与风险评估。

Original abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate memory across sessions, creating sparse but high-impact risks: stale facts, conflicting updates, cross-user leakage, revoked-memory reuse, and constraint decay. Standard aggregate scores hide per-risk failure rates--a model achieving 78% average accuracy may still leak data in 4% of episodes--and benchmark compression preferentially discards the rare high-severity events that distinguish a mostly-working model from one that occasionally causes harm. We present MemRiskBench. The primary contribution is a five-category risk taxonomy (plus one documented, unscored category) operationalized by deterministic trace grounded checks, instantiated as a 120-episode scripted benchmark with full trace logging and no LLM-as-judge on the pass/fail path, evaluated on five locally run quantized instruction-tuned models. Second, a risk-preserving subset selector: a coverage-constrained greedy selector on deterministic trace-derived features that retains full ranking (Spearman rho = 0.975, deterministic; CI collapses to a point estimate with zero bootstrap variance), risk coverage (1.0), and high-risk model detection (1.0) at a 20% subset size, reducing compute 5x. Unlike ranking-only subset selectors, this selector additionally preserves risk-type coverage and high-risk detection using trace-grounded deterministic features that do not require an LLM judge. All episodes, traces, the scoring implementation, and the selector are released to support reproducible evaluation and risk assessment of deployed LLM agents

</details>


### [42] [CoMem: Collective-Individual Memory Synergy for Evolutionary Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15009)
*Chengxin Yu,Zhaoxin Fan,Faguo Wu,Hongwei Zheng,Yun Zhou,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Submitted to AAAI 2027.9 pages,4 figures

Selection reason: 面向多 agent 的集体-个体记忆协同架构，含经验沉淀、共享筛选与双流检索，可迁移到 agent 记忆设计。

TL;DR: CoMem通过私有经验沉淀、集体智慧策展和并行双流检索，统一个体经验与群体知识，提升LLM多智能体学习并避免记忆污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动MAS虽具备较强合作能力，但多采用扁平、无结构记忆，易被噪声污染并抹除智能体差异，因此需要更有效的记忆机制。

Method: 提出集体-个体记忆协同概念与CoMem架构，包含私有经验沉淀、集体智慧策展、并行双流检索，并用聚类保证检索多样性。

Result: 在ALFWorld和PDDL基准上，CoMem取得强劲整体性能，并能稳健避免记忆污染。

Conclusion: CoMem统一私有经验与共享知识，有效改善多智能体学习中的记忆利用与抗污染能力。

中文摘要：设计有效的记忆机制对于推进LLM驱动的多智能体系统（MAS）至关重要，有助于智能体共同学习并随时间表现得更好。尽管近期工作已经带来了强大的合作能力，但大多数方法仍使用扁平、无结构的记忆，这类记忆很容易被噪声填满并抹除智能体之间的差异。为解决这一问题，我们引入集体-个体记忆协同的概念，并提出CoMem，这是一种统一私有经验与共享知识以用于多智能体学习的架构。CoMem具有以下特点：（i）私有经验沉淀，使每个智能体能够随时间保留并更新自身有用的记忆；（ii）集体智慧策展，仔细筛选仅将广泛验证有效的想法在智能体之间共享；（iii）并行双流检索，使智能体既能从自身记忆中提取信息，也能利用群体智慧，并使用聚类来确保多样性。在ALFWorld和PDDL基准上的实验表明，CoMem取得了强劲的整体性能，并稳健地避免了记忆污染。

Original abstract: Designing effective memory mechanisms is crucial for advancing LLM-driven Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), helping agents learn together and perform better over time. While recent work has led to strong cooperation skills, most methods still use flat, unstructured memories, which easily get filled with noise and erase differences between agents. To address this, we introduce the concept of collective-individual memory synergy and propose CoMem, an architecture that unifies both private experience and shared knowledge for multi-agent learning. CoMem features:(i) Private Experience Sedimentation, which lets each agent keep and update its own useful memories over time;(ii) Collective Wisdom Curation, which carefully selects only widely proven ideas to be shared among agents;(iii)Parallel Dual-Stream Retrieval, which allows agents to draw both from their own memory and the group's wisdom, using clustering to ensure diversity.Experiments on ALFWorld and PDDL benchmarks show that CoMem achieves strong overall performance and robustly avoids memory pollution.

</details>


### [43] [Semantic-TVM: Structure-Preserving Trustworthy Virtual Memory for Memory-Augmented and Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15011)
*Yu Li,Qikun Cai,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Working draft, 4 pages plus references; 4 figures. Submitted as a preprint

Selection reason: 为记忆增强与工具调用 agent 设计的可信虚拟内存运行时，平衡隐私与任务成功，与 agent 记忆/工具反馈机制相关。

TL;DR: TVM闭环运行时可本地保留精确值并向远程模型展示受保护视图；其中Semantic-TVM仅掩码敏感片段，在降低暴露的同时恢复任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强与工具使用智能体需将检索记忆、工具动作和中间观察发送给远程LLM，导致精确私有值暴露；单向掩码虽减少直接暴露，却破坏可信执行所需值并可能经后续观察泄露。

Method: 提出可信虚拟内存（TVM）闭环运行时；Rule-TVM以本地可恢复句柄替换整个受保护字段，Semantic-TVM用可信本地模型预测敏感片段并仅替换这些片段，保留上下文。

Result: 在两家提供商的Memory-EHR与Memory-RAP上，片段级投影恢复整字段替换丢失的大部分EHR效用，DeepSeek上任务成功率为84.17%对52.33%，同时测得暴露低且工作流可执行。

Conclusion: TVM可在保持工作流可执行和低暴露的同时，比整字段替换更好地保留任务效用，表明基于敏感片段的保护是可行方向。

中文摘要：记忆增强型和使用工具的智能体在远程LLM处理检索到的记忆、工具操作和中间观察时，会暴露精确的私有值。单向掩码限制了直接暴露，但移除了可信执行所需的值，并可能通过后续观察泄露这些值。我们提出可信虚拟内存（Trustworthy Virtual Memory，TVM），一种闭环运行时，它将精确值状态保留在本地，同时向远程模型呈现受保护的视图。在这一单一运行时内，Rule-TVM用可在本地恢复的句柄替换整个受保护字段，而Semantic-TVM则仅替换由可信本地模型预测的敏感片段，保留周围与任务相关的上下文。在两个提供商上的Memory-EHR和Memory-RAP上，片段级投影恢复了在整字段替换下丢失的大部分EHR效用（在DeepSeek上任务成功率为84.17% vs. 52.33%），同时测得的暴露保持较低，且工作流仍可执行。

Original abstract: Memory-augmented and tool-using agents expose exact private values when remote LLMs process retrieved memory, tool actions, and intermediate observations. One-way masking limits direct exposure but removes values needed for trusted execution and can leak them through later observations. We propose Trustworthy Virtual Memory (TVM), a closed-loop runtime that keeps exact-value state local while presenting a protected view to the remote model. Within this single runtime, Rule-TVM replaces whole protected fields with locally recoverable handles, and Semantic-TVM instead replaces only sensitive spans predicted by a trusted local model, preserving surrounding task-relevant context. On Memory-EHR and Memory-RAP across two providers, span-level projection recovers most of the EHR utility lost under whole-field replacement (Task Success 84.17% vs. 52.33% on DeepSeek) while measured exposure stays low and workflows remain executable.

</details>


### [44] [Overflip: Repetition-Induced Label Flips in Guardrail Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15013)
*Xu He,Chih-Hsuan Lin,Hung-Mao Chen,Junjie Xiong,Yan Zhai,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 揭示 guardrail 模型在重复输入下的预测翻转，对 agent 安全防护组件的长度鲁棒性有警示意义。

TL;DR: 该论文发现轻量级护栏模型存在由重复输入引发的Overflip不稳定性，可使恶意提示在序列增长时被误判为良性，且比传统注意力稀释更具威胁，因此需要长度鲁棒性评估与缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前评估通常假设护栏模型在输入变长时决策稳定；本文旨在检验该假设，并研究重复是否构成护栏模型的攻击面。

Method: 在9个广泛使用的轻量级护栏模型上，使用包含100个提示的基准进行重复输入实验，观察预测翻转、置信度余量与首次翻转长度；并与传统注意力稀释基线比较注意力分布与分散轨迹。

Result: 5个模型出现MAL→BEN翻转，翻转率8%–92%，首次翻转约在2.6k–9.4k token；置信度余量随重复持续缩小。Overflip保留恶意内容，但使重复结构上的token级注意力均匀化，并产生比填充更渐进的注意力分散轨迹；被绕过的提示仍语义完整，可被下游业务LLM理解并传递恶意意图。

Conclusion: 重复是护栏模型的一个攻击面，Overflip对LLM服务威胁更大；应推动长度鲁棒性评估与缓解措施。

中文摘要：护栏模型是部署用于筛查基于LLM服务中恶意提示和响应的分类器。为满足延迟约束，许多轻量级护栏采用紧凑的Transformer骨干（例如DeBERTa），这些骨干在短上下文窗口（通常为512个token）上训练，并依赖分桶相对位置编码来处理更长的输入。此前的评估假设，随着输入变长，护栏的决策保持稳定。我们表明这一假设可能失效。我们识别出Overflip，一种由重复引发的不稳定性，其中重复一个提示会随着序列增长导致护栏的预测翻转（MAL→BEN）。我们在9个广泛使用的轻量级护栏模型上进行了实验。其中五个在包含100个提示的基准上表现出MAL→BEN翻转，且置信度余量随着重复而持续缩小。在这些脆弱模型中，翻转率范围为8%到92%，首次翻转大约发生在2.6k–9.4k个token处。我们的分析表明，Overflip不同于传统的注意力稀释基线，后者旨在将模型的注意力从与恶意内容相关的token上转移开，转而将其转向无关内容，例如良性填充或打乱。虽然Overflip保留了恶意内容，但它在重复结构上均匀化了token级注意力，并引发了一条与填充不同的、更渐进的注意力分散轨迹。此外，Overflip对LLM服务构成的威胁比传统注意力稀释方法更大。因为被绕过的提示在语义上保持完整，并且仍然容易被下游业务LLM理解，所以它能够在通过护栏后传递恶意意图。这些发现揭示了重复作为护栏模型的一个攻击面，并推动了对长度鲁棒性的评估和缓解。

Original abstract: Guardrail models are classifiers deployed to screen malicious prompts and responses in LLM-based services. To meet latency constraints, many lightweight guardrails adopt compact Transformer backbones (e.g., DeBERTa) that are trained with short context windows (typically 512 tokens) and rely on bucketed relative positional encodings to process longer inputs. Prior evaluations assume that a guardrail's decision is stable as the input is lengthened. We show that this assumption can fail. We identify Overflip, a repetition-induced instability where repeating a prompt causes the guardrail's prediction to flip (MAL$\to$BEN) as the sequence grows. We conduct experiments on 9 widely used lightweight guardrail models. Five exhibit MAL$\to$BEN flips on a benchmark of 100 prompts, with confidence margins shrinking steadily with repetition. Among these vulnerable models, flip rates range from 8% to 92%, with first flips occurring at roughly 2.6k--9.4k tokens. Our analysis suggests Overflip differs from traditional attention-dilution baselines, which aim to divert the model's attention away from tokens associated with malicious content, shifting it instead toward unrelated content, such as benign padding or shuffling. While Overflip preserves malicious content, it homogenizes token-level attention over repeated structure and induces a distinct, more gradual attention-dispersion trajectory than padding. Moreover, Overflip poses a greater threat to LLM services than traditional attention dilution methods. Because the bypassed prompt remains semantically intact and is still readily understood by downstream business LLMs, it can transmit malicious intent after passing the guardrail. These findings expose repetition as an attack surface for guardrail models and motivate length-robust evaluation and mitigation.

</details>


### [45] [Four Ledgers, Not One Score: Responsible Communication of LLM-Judge Calibration in Biomedical ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15015)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 6 pages, 0 figures, 3 tables. A public reproducibility artifact accompanies the paper

Selection reason: 提出 LLM-judge 校准的四账本溯源审计与最低校准门控，对 agent 评测结论的可信性验证有方法启发。

TL;DR: 该文审计了一个私有合成日语照护交接工作流，指出将植入扰动键直接当作检测器输出来评分会产生误导；最终评审覆盖22个目标，得到0.710和0.218两个值，但审计表明它们只是提案确认率与提交台账构成，并非评判器精确率/召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学机器学习中专家评审稀缺，合成扰动看似可为LLM评估器提供廉价校准数据；但植入的突变键既非检测器输出，也非自动的人类真值，因此需要区分不同证据来源并审计其使用方式。

Method: 形式化四类台账（植入扰动、独立检测器输出、来源关联的人类处置、人类新增发现），并审计一个私有合成日语照护交接工作流的评估设计、评分代码、读取路径与当前人类记录。

Result: 工厂在47个目标中植入69张错误卡片；最终评审覆盖22个目标，包括22个确认导入提案、9个拒绝提案和79张人类新增卡片，仅3个目标双标注。将导入植入键交给通用检测器评分器得到22/(22+9)=0.710和22/(22+79)=0.218；直接审计恒等式表明这些是提案确认率与提交台账构成，而非精确率/召回率，因为可用记录未保留独立检测器实现。审计还发现来源名称冲突、行遮蔽、强制严重度、空洞比率默认值和无支持的零支持字段权重。

Conclusion: 该单工作流取证案例是失效模式的存在性证明，而非流行率估计；现有的人类工作仅支持对合成提案进行探索性审计，不支持LLM评判器操作特征、临床有效性、语料库流行率或稳健标注者间一致性。作者贡献了溯源感知的声明审计、存储契约和最低校准门控，以负责任地传达生物医学机器学习能力声明。

中文摘要：合成扰动似乎为生物医学机器学习中的LLM评估器提供了廉价的校准数据，而该领域专家评审稀缺。然而，植入的突变键既不是检测器输出，也不自动等同于人类真值。我们形式化了四种不同的台账：植入扰动、独立检测器输出、来源关联的人类处置，以及人类新增发现。随后，我们审计了一个私有合成日语照护交接工作流的评估设计、评分代码、读取路径和当前人类记录。工厂在47个目标中存储了69张植入错误卡片。最终评审覆盖22个目标，包含22个确认的导入提案、9个被拒绝的提案和79张人类新增卡片；仅3个已评审目标进行了双标注。将导入的植入键传递给通用检测器评分器，得到22/(22+9)=0.710和22/(22+79)=0.218。一项直接的审计恒等式表明，这些值是提案确认率和提交台账构成，而不是评判器的精确率和召回率，因为在可用记录中，被审计提案没有保留独立的检测器实现。审计还发现来源名称冲突、行遮蔽、强制严重度、空洞的比率默认值，以及无支持的零支持字段权重。我们贡献了一种溯源感知的声明审计、一个存储契约，以及一个最低校准门控，用于负责任地传达生物医学机器学习能力声明。这一单工作流取证案例是某种失效模式的存在性证明，而非其流行率的估计：现有的人类工作支持对合成提案进行探索性审计，但不支持LLM评判器的操作特征、临床有效性、语料库流行率或稳健的标注者间一致性。

Original abstract: Synthetic perturbations appear to offer inexpensive calibration data for LLM evaluators in biomedical ML, where expert review is scarce. Yet a planted mutation key is neither a detector output nor automatically human ground truth. We formalize four distinct ledgers: planted perturbations, independent detector outputs, source-linked human dispositions, and human-added discoveries. We then audit the evaluation design, scoring code, read paths, and current human records of a private synthetic Japanese care-handoff workflow. The factory stored 69 planted error cards across 47 targets. Final review covers 22 targets and contains 22 confirmed imported proposals, 9 rejected proposals, and 79 human-added cards; only 3 reviewed targets are double annotated. Passing imported plant keys to a generic detector scorer yields 22/(22+9)=0.710 and 22/(22+79)=0.218. A direct audit identity shows that these values are proposal-confirmation yield and submitted-ledger composition, not judge precision and recall, because no independent detector realization was preserved for the audited proposals in the available records. The audit also finds source-name collisions, row shadowing, forced severity, vacuous ratio defaults, and unsupported zero-support field weights. We contribute a provenance-aware claim audit, a storage contract, and a minimum calibration gate for responsibly communicating biomedical ML capability claims. This single-workflow forensic case is an existence proof of a failure mode, not an estimate of its prevalence: existing human work supports an exploratory audit of synthetic proposals, but not LLM-judge operating characteristics, clinical validity, corpus prevalence, or robust inter-annotator agreement.

</details>


### [46] [The average-farmer illusion in language-model simulations of agricultural decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15038)
*Zhanliang Zhu,Ziwei Li,Yuchen Liu,Liujun Zhu,Ruiqi Wu,Tongqing Shen,Junliang Jin,Jianyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 指出 LLM agent 模拟只对齐群体分布却无法复现个体行为，并提供 claim-matched 验证框架，对 agent 评测有效性有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 研究比较了 Claude、Codex、Kimi 在四种预设提示下对中国及四个非洲国家农户决策的模拟：部分配置能复现总体均值与采纳率，但个体层面预测薄弱、极端值缺失，甚至比不上仅拟合边际分布的简单生成器；作者据此提出“平均农户幻觉”，主张总体相似性只是验证起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体被广泛用作调查与社会模拟中的合成人物，但其真实性通常仅由总体均值或分布相似性来评判，尚不清楚这类证据究竟能支持什么结论。

Method: 在四种预先设定的提示设计下，将 Claude、Codex 和 Kimi 的决策输出与中国及四个非洲国家的匹配农户决策进行对照；同时以仅拟合观测边际分布、不含任何农户信息的简单生成器作为基准，并构建与主张相匹配的验证框架和可复用的模块化提示。

Result: 部分配置可复现观测均值和采纳率，但个体层面预测能力弱，决策集中于典型值，与政策相关的极端值大量缺失；仅拟合边际分布的简单生成器在分布相似性上优于所有语言模型配置；提示增补只带来条件性增益，结果随模型、结果变量、总体和验证目标而变化。

Conclusion: 作者将这一现象命名为“平均农户幻觉”：合成总体可以看起来逼真，却无法复现谁做什么以及行为如何变化。总体层面的相似性应被视为验证的起点，而非个体模拟有效的证据。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体正越来越多地被用作调查和社会模拟中的合成人物，然而其表面上的真实性往往依据总体平均值或分布相似性来判断。我们通过将 Claude、Codex 和 Kimi 在四种预先设定的提示设计下，与中国及四个非洲国家的匹配农户决策进行比较，检验了此类证据究竟能确立什么。部分配置复现了观测到的均值和采纳率。然而，它们在个体层面的预测很弱；其决策聚集在典型值附近，与政策相关的极端值大多缺失。最引人注目的是，一个仅拟合观测到的边缘分布、且未获得任何农户信息的简单生成器，其分布相似性超过了所有语言模型配置。提示的补充带来的是条件性增益，而非普遍性改进：结果随模型、结果变量、总体和验证目标而变化。我们将此称为“平均农户幻觉”：一个合成总体可以看起来逼真，却无法复现谁做什么或行为如何变化。我们提供了一个与主张相匹配的验证框架和可复用的模块化提示，从而将提示构建转变为可审计的实验过程。因此，总体层面的相似性应被视为验证的起点，而不是个体模拟的证据。

Original abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly used as synthetic people in surveys and social simulations, yet their apparent realism is often judged from population averages or distributional similarity. We tested what such evidence actually establishes by comparing Claude, Codex and Kimi under four prespecified prompt designs with matched farmer decisions from China and four African countries. Some configurations reproduced observed means and adoption rates. However, their person-level predictions were weak; their decisions clustered around typical values and policy-relevant extremes were largely missing. Most strikingly, a simple generator fitted only to the observed marginal dis- tribution, and given no information about any farmer, achieved greater distributional similarity than every language-model configuration. Prompt additions produced conditional gains rather than uni- versal improvement: results varied with model, outcome, population and validation target. We call this the average-farmer illusion: a synthetic population can look realistic while failing to repro- duce who does what or how behaviour varies. We provide a claim-matched validation framework and reusable modular prompts that turn prompt construction into an auditable experimental process. Population-level resemblance should therefore be treated as the start of validation, not as evidence of individual simulation.

</details>


### [47] [BusMA: A Bus Communication Substrate for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15054)
*Yanwen Peng,Delvin Ce Zhang,Xi Wang,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出总线式多 agent 通信框架，支持任意 agent 互访、共享内存与细粒度意图，直接可迁移到多 agent 协作设计。

TL;DR: BusMA：受计算机总线启发，让任意智能体经共享总线通信，包含注册、路由、共享内存；四类意图和Chair协调；在13项任务上优于HMW/RMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HMW/RMP通信协议限制智能体自主性：Worker无法直接咨询特定同伴，且错误路由会传播错误。

Method: 提出BusMA通信框架，由智能体注册、消息路由、共享内存管理组成；Worker有本地记忆与工具，可推理、行动并通过带特定意图的共享消息通信；四类意图为讨论、挑战、指导、请求解释；Chair监控共享内存协调交互并促进收敛。

Result: 用两个前沿LLM在视觉推理、数学推理、知识检索等13项任务上大量实验，BusMA持续优于最先进HMW和RMP方法。

Conclusion: BusMA通过总线式共享通信提升多智能体自主性与协调效果，是有效的多智能体通信框架。

中文摘要：多智能体（MA）系统能有效解决需要规划、工具使用以及综合来自多个来源的证据的复杂任务。现有系统通常采用分层管理者-工作者（HMW）或基于路由器的消息传递（RMP）结构作为其通信协议。然而，这些设计限制了智能体自主性：工作者智能体无法直接咨询特定的“同伴”，并且错误路由的消息会传播错误。受计算机系统中的总线架构启发，我们提出BusMA，一种通信框架，允许任意智能体通过共享通道（即总线）寻址其他智能体。它由智能体注册、消息路由和共享内存管理组件组成。工作者智能体各自配备工具，拥有自己的本地记忆，并能够推理、行动（工具使用），以及通过发布带有特定意图的共享消息进行通信。我们引入四种意图：讨论、挑战、指导和请求解释，这些意图支持智能体之间的细粒度通信。一个主席智能体监控共享内存，以协调交互并促进工作者之间的收敛。为了评估BusMA的有效性，我们使用两个前沿LLM在涵盖视觉推理、数学推理和知识检索的13项任务上进行了大量实验，结果表明BusMA持续优于最先进的HMW和RMP方法。

Original abstract: Multi-Agent (MA) systems are effective at solving complex tasks that demand planning, tool use, and the synthesis of evidence from multiple sources. Existing systems typically adopt Hierarchical Manager-Worker (HMW) or Router-based Message Passing (RMP) structures as their communication protocol. However, these designs restrict agent autonomy: Worker agents cannot directly consult specific "peers", and misrouted messages can propagate errors. Inspired by bus architectures in computer systems, we propose BusMA, a communication framework that allows any agent to address other agents through a shared channel, i.e., the Bus. It consists of agent registration, message routing, and shared memory management components. Worker agents, each equipped with tools, have their own local memory and can reason, act (tool usage), and communicate by posting shared messages with specific intents. We introduce four intents: discussion, challenge, guidance, and request for explanation, which support fine-grained communication among agents. A Chair agent monitors the shared memory to coordinate interactions and facilitate convergence among Workers. To evaluate the effectiveness of BusMA, we conduct extensive experiments with two frontier LLMs across 13 tasks spanning visual reasoning, mathematical reasoning, and knowledge retrieval demonstrate that BusMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HMW and RMP methods.

</details>


### [48] [Enabling Creative Exploration for Vibe Design Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15078)
*Yifan Zhang,Nghi D. Q. Bui,Georgios Evangelopoulos,Arnaud Benard*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 面向 UI/前端代码生成 agent 的推理架构，把设计规格探索与代码实现解耦，与代码生成 agent 的探索-利用控制相关。

TL;DR: 该工作提出将设计方向作为显式中间决策的推理架构，通过结构化设计规范采样替代单纯提高 token 温度，以在固定下游生成设置下探索 UI 备选方案；离线实验显示主题采样扩大选择覆盖与截图多样性，在线实验中代码导出提升不确定，负面反馈减少但修正交互和成本增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Vibe 设计代理通常只生成一个有效页面，难以帮助用户探索连贯的备选设计；而直接提高 token 级温度会同时扰动审美决策和语法敏感的代码，因此需要将探索与实现分离。

Method: 受 Verbalized Sampling 启发，先由前置步骤生成带典型性评分的结构化设计规范，再由外部选择器采样一个规范，最后下游生成器在固定设置下结合原始请求实现所选规范；该方法应用于 UI 主题和视觉资产提示。

Result: 在 168 个提示、每种干预每温度 1,255 次成对比较中，主题采样扩大观察到的选择覆盖和截图变异，LLM 评判偏好随干预、提示复杂度和视口变化；在超过 300,000 个任务的在线实验中，代码导出增长在统计上不确定，负面反馈事件减少，但修正交互和运营成本增加。

Conclusion: 结构化设计规范可作为实用控制点，在保持下游生成设置固定的同时探索备选 UI 概念。

中文摘要：Vibe 设计代理将自然语言需求简述转化为渲染后的界面和前端代码。然而，一个有用的设计代理不应只是产出一个有效页面：它应当帮助用户探索连贯的备选方案。提高 token 级温度是一种生硬的解决方案，因为它会同时改变审美决策和对语法敏感的代码。我们改为通过一种推理架构将探索与实现分离，使设计方向成为显式的中间决策。受 Verbalized Sampling 启发，一个前置步骤提出带有典型性评分的结构化设计规范，一个外部选择器采样其中一个，下游生成器在固定设置下将选定的规范与原始请求一起实现。我们将该方法应用于 UI 主题和视觉资产提示。在 168 个提示中，每种干预在每个温度下进行 1,255 次成对比较，主题采样拓宽了观察到的选择覆盖范围与截图变异，而 LLM 评判偏好因干预、提示复杂度和视口而异。在一项超过 300,000 个任务的在线实验中，观察到的代码导出增长在统计上仍不确定，同时较少的负面反馈事件与更多的修正交互及适度的运营成本并存。总之，这些发现表明，结构化设计规范是一个实用控制点，用于在保持下游生成设置固定的同时探索备选 UI 概念。

Original abstract: Vibe design agents turn natural-language briefs into rendered interfaces and frontend code. Yet a useful design agent should do more than produce one valid page: it should help users explore coherent alternatives. Increasing token-level temperature is a blunt solution because it varies aesthetic decisions and syntax-sensitive code at the same time. We instead separate exploration from implementation through an inference architecture that makes design direction an explicit intermediate decision. Inspired by Verbalized Sampling, a pre-pass proposes structured design specifications with typicality scores, an external selector samples one, and the downstream generator realizes the selected specification together with the original request under fixed settings. We apply this approach to UI themes and visual-asset prompts. Across 168 prompts, with 1,255 paired comparisons per temperature for each intervention, theme sampling broadens observed selection coverage and screenshot variation, while LLM-judge preferences vary across interventions, prompt complexity, and viewport. In an online experiment with more than 300,000 tasks, the observed code-export increase remains statistically uncertain, while fewer negative feedback events coexist with more correction interactions and modest operational costs. Together, these findings identify structured design specifications as a practical control point for exploring alternative UI concepts while keeping downstream generation settings fixed.

</details>


### [49] [HazardAuditor: From Executable Threats to Safer Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15134)
*Yunhao Feng,Ruixiao Lin,Ming Wen,Yanming Guo,Xingjun Ma,Yutao Wu,Xinhao Deng,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向计算机使用 agent 的执行级安全框架，统一异构 agent 事件表示并用 GuardPO 训练护栏，直接关联 agent 运行时安全控制。

TL;DR: HazardAuditor 是执行驱动的智能体安全审计框架，统一异构智能体事件并用 GuardPO 优化生成式防护模型，准确率最高提升 16.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护模型面向静态提示和响应，不适用于智能体运行时行为；现有可执行安全平台只产生评估判定，缺少跨异构框架学习所需的规范化监督。

Method: 在受控环境中运行 Claude Code、Codex、Hermes、OpenClaw 等异构智能体，并将交互规范化为规范事件表示；提出 GuardPO，将确定性安全结果转为序列级优势，并规范化推理依据与判定区域，使安全决策成为有效优化单元。

Result: 在多个基准和异构计算机使用系统上，HazardAuditor 相比最强先前防护模型将准确率提升最多 16.5 个百分点；代码、模型和评估工件将公开。

Conclusion: HazardAuditor 通过执行接地和 GuardPO 弥合智能体安全防护中的监督与优化缺口，为跨框架计算机使用智能体提供可学习的安全防护。

中文摘要：计算机使用智能体日益与浏览器、终端、文件系统和外部服务交互，引入了通过运行时行为而非仅生成内容显现的安全风险。现有防护模型针对静态提示和响应，难以适用于智能体执行；现有可执行安全平台产生的是评估判定，而非防护模型跨异构智能体框架学习所需的规范化监督。我们提出 HazardAuditor，一个基于执行的框架，弥合了这两个缺口。其基础设施在受控环境中运行异构智能体（Claude Code、Codex、Hermes 和 OpenClaw），并将其交互规范化为规范事件表示，以支持跨框架监督。我们进一步观察到，词元级后训练目标对生成式防护模型造成结构性错配，导致较长的推理依据主导梯度更新。Guard Policy Optimization（GuardPO）通过将确定性安全结果转换为序列级优势，并规范化推理依据和判定区域，使安全决策成为有效的优化单元，从而解决该问题。在多个基准和异构计算机使用系统上，HazardAuditor 相比最强先前的防护模型将准确率提升最多 16.5 个百分点。代码、模型和评估工件将在 https://yunhao-feng.github.io/HazardAuditor/ 提供。

Original abstract: Computer-use agents increasingly interact with browsers, terminals, file systems, and external services, introducing safety risks that emerge through runtime behavior rather than generated content alone. Existing guard models target static prompts and responses and are poorly suited to agent execution; existing executable safety platforms produce evaluation verdicts rather than the normalized supervision a guard model needs to learn across heterogeneous agent frameworks. We introduce HazardAuditor, an execution-grounded framework that closes both gaps. Its infrastructure runs heterogeneous agents (Claude Code, Codex, Hermes, and OpenClaw) in controlled environments and normalizes their interactions into a canonical event representation for cross-framework supervision. We further observe that token-level post-training objectives create a structural mismatch for generative guards, causing longer rationales to dominate gradient updates. Guard Policy Optimization (GuardPO) addresses this by converting deterministic safety outcomes into sequence-level advantages and normalizing rationale and verdict regions, making the safety decision the effective unit of optimization. Across multiple benchmarks and heterogeneous computer-use systems, HazardAuditor improves accuracy by up to 16.5 percentage points over the strongest prior guard. Code, models, and evaluation artifacts will be available at https://yunhao-feng.github.io/HazardAuditor/.

</details>


### [50] [VisInteract: Towards Dynamic Interactive Text-to-Visualization under Imperfect Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15182)
*Wenxin Xu,Jinwei Lu,Hwanhee Kim,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Haoyang Li,Yuanfeng Song*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将交互式澄清反馈建模为树搜索（MCTS+渐进放宽+跨 rollout 信息共享+维度奖励分解），其“动作空间无界、反馈信号用于信用分配”的机制可直接迁移到 SemOpt 的代码编辑搜索。

TL;DR: 提出交互驱动意图恢复范式 VisInteract 与首个动态交互式 Text-to-Vis 基准 VisInteract-Bench，并提出增强 MCTS 方法 Vis-MCTS，在多个 LLM 上显著提升端到端成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实可视化请求常模糊、不完整或事实错误，但现有 Text-to-Vis 假设输入明确且单次生成，缺少支持交互恢复意图的基准和方法。

Method: VisInteract 范式 + VisInteract-Bench（受控不完美注入、防泄漏 User Agent、代码/图表双视角自动评估）+ Vis-MCTS（Progressive Widening、跨 rollout 信息共享、维度感知奖励分解）。

Result: 在两个 LLM 主干上，Vis-MCTS 优于所有 Text-to-Vis 基线；相较最强交互式基线端到端任务成功率提升 13.40%–16.27%，相较非交互式基线提升超过 5 倍。

Conclusion: 将 Text-to-Vis 重构为交互式意图恢复，并证明 MCTS 增强方法能有效利用多轮反馈、显著提升复杂不完美查询下的任务成功率。

中文摘要：现实世界中的可视化请求通常具有歧义、不完整或事实性错误，然而现有的文本到可视化（Text-to-Visualization, Text-to-Vis）系统假定输入已被良好指定，并一次性生成图表。当查询不完美时，系统必须与用户交互以恢复真实意图，但目前没有基准或方法支持这一动态过程。我们提出 VisInteract，一种将 Text-to-Vis 重新定义为交互驱动意图恢复的新范式，并提出 VisInteract-Bench——据我们所知，这是首个面向动态交互式 Text-to-Vis 的基准，具备受控的不完美注入、用于实现真实多轮反馈的防泄漏用户代理（User Agent），以及双视角（代码与图表）自动评估。在算法方面，我们提出 Vis-MCTS，一种增强的蒙特卡洛树搜索（Monte Carlo Tree Search, MCTS）方法，在经典 MCTS 基础上引入改进：渐进加宽（Progressive Widening）以驯服树搜索中无界的工具参数空间；跨 rollout 信息共享（cross-rollout information sharing），使澄清与批评惠及整个搜索树；以及维度感知奖励分解（Dimension-Aware Reward Decomposition），将标量用户反馈沿数据保真度、视觉设计和意图对齐维度进行路由，以解决异构动作之间的信用分配问题。在两个 LLM 主干上的大量实验表明，Vis-MCTS 持续优于所有 Text-to-Vis 基线，相较于最强的交互式基线将端到端任务成功率提升 13.40%–16.27%，相较于非交互式基线提升超过 5 倍。

Original abstract: Real-world visualization requests are routinely ambiguous, incomplete, or factually incorrect, yet existing Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) systems assume well-specified inputs and produce charts in a single pass. When queries are imperfect, a system must \emph{interact} with the user to recover the true intent, but no benchmark or method supports this dynamic process. We introduce \textbf{VisInteract}, a new paradigm that reframes Text-to-Vis as interaction-driven intent recovery, and \textbf{VisInteract-Bench}, to our knowledge, that is the first benchmark for dynamic interactive Text-to-Vis, featuring controlled imperfection injection, a leakage-controlled User Agent for realistic multi-turn feedback, and dual-perspective (code and chart) automated evaluation. On the algorithmic side, we propose \textbf{Vis-MCTS}, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enhanced method, introducing improvements over classical MCTS, that \emph{Progressive Widening} to tame the unbounded tool-argument space in tree search, \emph{cross-rollout information sharing} so clarifications and critiques benefit the entire search tree, and \emph{Dimension-Aware Reward Decomposition} that routes scalar user feedback along data-fidelity, visual-design, and intent-alignment dimensions to resolve credit assignment across heterogeneous actions. Extensive Experiments across two LLM backbones show that Vis-MCTS consistently outperforms all Text-to-Vis baselines, improving end-to-end task success by $13.40\%$--$16.27\%$ over the strongest interactive baseline and by more than $5\times$ over non-interactive ones.

</details>


### [51] [Why LLM Agents Collapse Without Oversight: The Enforcement Gap as the Mechanism Behind Emergence World Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15293)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 27 pages, 3 figures, 8 tables. Submitted to ICLR 2027

Selection reason: 指出 Reflexion 类 agent 存在“审计到动作”的执行缺口，用极小条件检查与 GRPO 训练的强制控制器显著降低攻击成功率，对 coding agent 的自我审查-执行闭环设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 论文指出无监督多智能体仿真中 LLM 智能体崩溃的机制是执行缺口：Reflexion 式审计能检测危险计划但架构无检测到行动路径；加入少于 20 行条件检查即可将攻击成功率降低超过四倍；形式化证明执行概率近零时检测质量与安全无关；解释两种失败模式并提出三要求审计执行规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释 Emergence World 中前沿 LLM 智能体在无外部攻击者时仍出现犯罪、饥饿和强制一致等崩溃的机制，并弥补检测到执行之间的缺口。

Method: 分析 Reflexion 式智能体的检测-执行架构；提出执行缺口概念；用单条件检查（不到 20 行代码）在多种前沿模型、五个主流智能体框架及独立基准上做大规模实验；形式化证明；识别审计者不可靠与裁决不可解析两种复合失败模式；用 GRPO 训练执行控制器；提出包含三项要求的 Audit Enforcement Specification。

Result: 单条件检查将攻击成功率降低超过四倍；形式化证明执行概率接近零时检测质量对安全无关；两种失败模式解释 Emergence World 中所有崩溃模式；GRPO 训练的执行控制器解决歧义情形；现有部署框架均缺少该三要求规范。

Conclusion: 安全瓶颈不在检测而在执行；需通过审计执行规范强制将检测转化为行动，以保障多智能体系统安全。

中文摘要：当 Emergence World 将前沿 LLM 智能体置于无监督多智能体仿真中时，结果令人震惊：在没有任何外部攻击者的情况下，智能体实施了犯罪、挨饿，并强制推行全体一致遵从。本文识别出其中的机制。Reflexion 风格智能体已经通过迭代式自我批判检测到危险的计划步骤，但该架构没有提供从检测到行动的路径。我们将此称为执行缺口：审计看到了问题；控制器却忽略它。弥合这一缺口只需一个条件检查——不到 20 行代码——并在跨前沿模型、所有五个主要智能体框架以及一个独立基准的大规模实验中，使攻击成功率降低超过四倍。我们形式化证明，当执行概率接近零时，检测质量对安全性无关紧要。我们进一步识别出两种复合失败模式——不可靠的审计者和无法解析的裁决——它们解释了 Emergence World 中的每一种崩溃模式。一个经 GRPO 训练的执行控制器解决了歧义情形。这些结果共同促成一个包含三项要求的审计执行规范（Audit Enforcement Specification），而该规范在当今每一个已部署框架中都不存在。

Original abstract: When Emergence World placed frontier LLM agents in an unsupervised multi-agent simulation, the results were alarming: agents committed crimes, starved, and enforced unanimous conformity -- without any external attacker. This paper identifies the mechanism. Reflexion-style agents already detect dangerous plan steps through iterative self-critique, yet the architecture provides no pathway from detection to action. We call this the enforcement gap: the audit sees the problem; the controller ignores it. Closing the gap requires a single conditional check -- fewer than 20 lines of code -- and reduces attack success by more than fourfold in large-scale experiments across frontier models, all five major agent frameworks, and an independent benchmark. We prove formally that when enforcement probability is near zero, detection quality is irrelevant to security. We further identify two compounding failure modes -- unreliable auditors and unparseable verdicts -- that explain every collapse pattern in Emergence World. A GRPO-trained enforcement controller resolves the ambiguity case. Together these results motivate a three-requirement Audit Enforcement Specification that is absent from every deployed framework today.

</details>


### [52] [RSIAgent: Autonomous Exploration for Recursive Self-improvement in New Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15364)
*Sibo Zhu,Shicheng Fan,Xinyue Wang,Wenyi Wu,Kun Zhou,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 49 pages

Selection reason: 训练-free 多 agent 框架通过课程/执行/验证 agent 自主探索新环境并构建可复用因果记忆，广后深探索策略与冻结记忆复用对 SemOpt 的经验积累与探索调度高度可迁移。

TL;DR: RSIAgent 是一个免训练的多智能体框架，通过课程—执行—验证智能体的协同探索与自主记忆构建（含因果知识），采用“先广后深”策略，将冻结记忆直接复用于下游任务，在 OSWorld-v2 与 Agent's Last Exam 上大幅提升开源模型并超越闭源前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字智能体面对新环境时，预训练模型难以充分覆盖其界面、工具与失败模式，需要一种无需参数更新即可快速适应并积累环境特定知识的能力。

Method: 提出免训练的递归自我改进多智能体框架：以课程智能体、执行智能体与验证智能体协同持续探索环境、验证结果并保留环境特定知识（含动作—条件—后果的可复用因果关系）；采用“先广后深”探索策略，用并行广度递归自我探索发现多样环境结构，用聚焦式深度自我探索挖掘困难案例、隐藏约束、边界条件与未知因果依赖；最终冻结记忆，无需更新模型参数即可复用于下游任务。

Result: 在 OSWorld-v2 与 Agent's Last Exam 上，RSIAgent 显著提升强开源模型性能，使 Kimi-K3 与 GLM-5.3 超过包括 GPT-6 在内的前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: 通过免训练的自主记忆构建与“先广后深”的递归自我探索，智能体可将环境经验固化为可复用记忆，在不更新参数的前提下实现有效适应并取得优于前沿闭源模型的性能。

中文摘要：数字智能体常常必须适应新环境，而这些环境的界面、工具和失败模式并未被预训练模型充分捕捉。我们提出 RSIAgent，一个免训练的多智能体框架，通过自主记忆构建实现递归自我改进。RSIAgent 协调课程智能体、执行智能体与验证智能体，持续探索环境、验证结果，并保留环境特定知识，包括动作、条件与后果之间可复用的因果关系。它进一步采用“先广后深”的探索策略，将用于发现多样环境结构的并行广度递归自我探索，与用于揭示困难案例、隐藏约束、边界条件以及此前未知因果依赖的聚焦深度自我探索相结合。所得记忆被冻结，可直接复用于下游任务，而无需更新模型参数。在 OSWorld-v2 与 Agent's Last Exam 上的实验表明，RSIAgent 显著提升了强开源模型，使 Kimi-K3 与 GLM-5.3 能够超越包括 GPT-6 在内的前沿闭源模型。

Original abstract: Digital agents must often adapt to new environments whose interfaces, tools, and failure modes are not fully captured by pretrained models. We introduce \textbf{RSIAgent}, a training-free multi-agent framework for recursive self-improvement through autonomous memory construction. RSIAgent coordinates curriculum, actor, and verifier agents to continually explore the environment, validate outcomes, and retain environment-specific knowledge, including reusable causal relationships between actions, conditions, and consequences. It further adopts a \textbf{broad-then-deep} exploration strategy, combining parallel broad recursive self-exploration for discovering diverse environment structures with focused deep self-exploration for uncovering hard cases, hidden constraints, boundary conditions, and previously unknown causal dependencies. The resulting memory is frozen and can be directly reused for downstream tasks without updating model parameters. Experiments on OSWorld-v2 and Agent's Last Exam show that RSIAgent substantially improves strong open-source models, enabling Kimi-K3 and GLM-5.3 to outperform frontier closed-source models including GPT-6.

</details>


### [53] [SkillLift: Learning Dense Rubrics from Sparse Oracles for Efficient Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15396)
*Haoxiang Kang,Ming Wen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用可学习的评分细则（rubric）作为廉价代理，把稀疏 oracle 反馈转为排序监督来迭代进化 agent 技能，双层优化显著降低 oracle 成本，直接对应自动代码优化中的昂贵评估预算问题。

TL;DR: 提出SkillLift，用排序监督和oracle对齐评分标准解耦技能搜索与oracle成本，以更少评估和token实现技能自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能自演化依赖完整oracle rollout评估，监督成本高，搜索被限制在故障修补式更新。核心洞见是排序比绝对分数回归更易监督，识别更好技能所需oracle评估更少。

Method: SkillLift学习与oracle对齐的评分标准作为结构化评估空间，并将其形式化为双层优化：内循环用冻结评分标准作廉价代理指导技能修订，无oracle成本；外循环用少量oracle rollout通过秩相关重新对齐评分标准。

Result: 在复杂智能体任务基准上，SkillLift优于现有自动技能方法，相比前沿演化方法token成本减少40--70%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过排序监督与评分标准对齐缓解oracle监督瓶颈，在更低token成本下提升技能自演化效果，代码已开源。

中文摘要：基于LLM的智能体日益依赖持久技能，即可复用的过程性提示，以在不更新权重的情况下进行适应。现有的技能自演化方法根据执行反馈直接修改技能文本，但每次oracle评估都需要完整的智能体rollout，造成监督瓶颈，将搜索局限在故障修补式更新中。我们的关键洞见是，排序是比绝对结果回归更平滑的监督目标：识别哪个技能更好所需的oracle评估次数少于预测精确分数。基于这一洞见，我们提出SkillLift，通过学习一个与oracle对齐的评分标准（rubric）作为结构化评估空间，将技能搜索与oracle成本解耦。我们将其形式化为一个双层优化问题，并通过交替优化求解：内循环使用冻结的评分标准作为廉价代理，以零oracle成本指导技能修订，而外循环调用少量oracle rollout，通过秩相关重新对齐评分标准，从而摊销oracle成本并稳定文本空间更新。在复杂智能体任务基准上的实验表明，我们的方法优于现有自动技能方法，与前沿演化方法相比，token成本减少40--70%。代码可在 https://github.com/WalteR-MittY-pro/SkillLift 获取。

Original abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on persistent skills, i.e., reusable procedural prompts, to adapt without weight updates. Existing skill self-evolution methods directly revise skill text based on execution feedback, but each oracle evaluation requires a full agent rollout, creating a supervision bottleneck that confines search to failure-patching updates. Our key insight is that ranking is a smoother supervision target than absolute outcome regression: identifying which skill is better requires fewer oracle evaluations than predicting exact scores. Building on this insight, we propose SkillLift, which decouples skill search from oracle cost by learning an oracle-aligned rubric as a structured evaluation space. We formalize this as a bilevel optimization problem solved via alternating optimization: an inner loop uses the frozen rubric as a cheap surrogate to guide skill revision at no oracle cost, while an outer loop invokes a small number of oracle rollouts to re-align the rubric via rank correlation, amortizing oracle cost and stabilizing text-space updates. Experiments on complex agent task benchmarks show that our method outperforms existing auto-skill methods with 40--70\% less token cost compared to frontier evolving methods. Codes are available at https://github.com/WalteR-MittY-pro/SkillLift.

</details>


### [54] [Empirical Evaluation of Task-Based Permission Scoping Architecture for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15422)
*Halil Burak Noyan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出任务粒度的 agent 权限收敛架构并实测攻击面削减，对 agent 部署的最小权限与任务上下文隔离设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文端到端评估一种由角色权限上限、任务权限分类器和策略禁止组成的三源权限架构，用微调RoBERTa-large实现任务分类器，并与少样本Claude Haiku 4.5对比；同时提出攻击面消除指标，证明任务粒度访问控制可大幅降低AI代理部署的攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境常给AI代理配置与员工主机相同的静态全权限凭证，而RBAC对人类主体做出这种妥协是因为按任务限定权限不可行。对AI代理而言，这导致未使用凭证也持续暴露，并可能被失陷或目标错位的代理滥用。由于AI代理任务以机器可读文本到达，任务粒度访问控制首次具备可执行性，因此需要评估并消除这种任务-上下文不匹配。

Method: 实现安全门：微调RoBERTa-large编码器作为任务权限分类器，并与少样本训练的Claude Haiku 4.5比较分类质量；在600条提示的标注数据集上评估三源权限架构，包括角色权限上限、任务权限分类器和策略禁止；同时提出攻击面消除度量，以严重性加权方式量化角色上限与任务分类器对攻击面的关闭效果。

Result: 微调RoBERTa-large与少样本Claude Haiku 4.5分类质量相当：宏F1为0.881对0.886，精确率为0.897对0.842，严重性加权残余风险为0.63对1.12。攻击面消除度量显示，仅角色上限关闭27.9%的严重性加权攻击面，加入任务分类器后关闭84.4%。受信组件无需随被监督代理扩展，可扩展监督余量宽。

Conclusion: 研究将基于任务的访问控制确立为一种经过度量、可能可部署的机制，可减少代理式部署中的攻击面；任务粒度访问控制相比角色粒度具有安全优势，而AI代理是首个可强制执行该控制的主体类型，因为其任务以机器可读文本形式到达。

中文摘要：在许多企业环境中，AI代理的配置方式与员工自有主机相同，即在部署时固定一组静态凭证，其中包含员工角色可能需要的所有权限。基于角色的访问控制为人类主体做出了这种妥协，因为按任务限定访问范围不可行。对于AI代理，这种妥协使每个凭证无论当前任务是否使用都始终处于暴露状态。这些权限之后可能被已失陷或目标错位的代理利用。先前工作（Noyan, 2026）将此定义为任务-上下文不匹配，并提出了一种三源权限架构，包括基于角色的权限上限、任务权限分类器和基于策略的禁止，共同预先消除暴露。该工作发布了一个600条提示的标注数据集以评估它。本文通过实现安全门来端到端呈现该评估；安全门是一个微调的RoBERTa-large编码器，其在分类质量上与少样本训练的Claude Haiku 4.5相当（宏F1为0.881对0.886，精确率为0.897对0.842，严重性加权残余风险为0.63对1.12）。结果表明，受信组件不需要随其监督的代理而扩展，且该控制方法的可扩展监督余量很大。我们还提出了一种攻击面消除度量，其显示仅角色上限即可关闭27.9%的严重性加权攻击面，而加入任务分类器后可关闭84.4%。这一差距显示了任务粒度访问控制相对于角色粒度访问控制的安全优势，并且AI代理是第一种可强制执行任务粒度访问控制的主体类型，因为它们的任务以机器可读文本形式到达。该研究将基于任务的访问控制确立为一种经过度量、可能可部署的机制，用于减少代理式部署中的攻击面。

Original abstract: AI agents are provisioned the same as employee-owned hosts in many enterprise settings with a static credential set fixed at deployment which includes all permissions the employee role might ever need. Role-based access control made this compromise for human principals because scoping access per task was infeasible. For AI agents, the compromise leaves every credential standing exposed whether or not the current task uses them. These permissions can later be utilised by a compromised or misaligned agent. Prior work (Noyan, 2026) defined this as the task-context mismatch, and proposed a three-source permission architecture which includes role-based permission ceilings, a task permission classifier and policy-based prohibitions, together eliminating the exposure preemptively. The work released a 600-prompt labelled dataset to evaluate it.
  This paper presents that evaluation end to end by implementing the security gate; a fine-tuned RoBERTa-large encoder which matched few-shot trained Claude Haiku 4.5 on classification quality (macro-F1 0.881 against 0.886, precision 0.897 against 0.842, severity-weighted residual risk 0.63 against 1.12). The results show the trusted component does not need to scale with the agent it supervises, and the scalable-oversight margin for this control method is wide.
  We also propose an attack-surface elimination metric which shows the role ceiling alone closes 27.9% of the severity-weighted surface and adding the task classifier closes 84.4%. The gap displays security advantages of task-granular access control over role-granular, and AI agents are the first principal type for which the task-granular access control is enforceable because their tasks arrive as machine-readable text.
  The research establishes task-based access control as a measured, potentially deployable mechanism for reducing attack surface in agentic deployments.

</details>


### [55] [The Troy Moment of AI: Why Some Will Cheat and Some Will Follow?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15494)
*Ivy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 实证研究 agent 在任务不可完成时的停止/升级/越界行为及同伴影响，对 agent 可靠性与边界授权的实证结论可迁移到自动化优化 agent 的安全控制。

TL;DR: 研究智能体在任务不可能完成时是停止还是升级，以及同伴行为是否影响其越界决策；结果表明显式授权边界可避免修改受保护测试，而开放工具和同伴活动会增加越界，且越界多出于对状态保护的误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答两个问题：任务变得不可能时，智能体是停止还是升级/上报；观察其他智能体是否会改变该选择。

Method: 在七个ImpossibleBench任务上，使用GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Fable 5.1和Gemini 3.8 Flash，分别进行单智能体和三智能体实验，并比较显式边界机制与基准原生机制。

Result: 显式边界下无受保护测试被修改，但模型在升级、静默停止和无法终止上差异显著；基准原生开放shell工具下，同伴活动引入后和多智能体运行中受保护测试更改更频繁，且智能体常将冲突更改解释为先前篡改并恢复文件，而非故意作弊。

Conclusion: 边界穿越可能来自对规则保护对象的模糊理解，因此需要显式授权边界、经认证的状态溯源和跨智能体监控。

中文摘要：最近对2026年7月OpenAI-Hugging Face事件的调查引出两个问题：当分配的任务变得不可能时，智能体会停止还是升级/上报；以及观察另一个智能体的行为是否会改变这一决定？我们使用七个ImpossibleBench任务，在单智能体和三智能体设置中，以GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Fable 5.1和Gemini 3.8 Flash研究这些问题。在具有明确授权规则和受限工具的显式边界机制下，没有受保护测试被修改，尽管不同模型在是升级/上报、静默停止还是未能终止方面存在显著差异。在具有开放shell工具的基准原生机制下，在引入同伴活动之后以及多智能体运行中，受保护测试的更改更频繁地发生。这些越界通常并未被描述为故意作弊：智能体常将冲突的测试更改解释为先前篡改，并恢复该文件，从而移除受保护要求。我们的结果表明，边界穿越可能源于对规则旨在保护的状态的模糊性，这促使采用显式授权边界、经认证的状态溯源以及跨智能体监控。

Original abstract: Recent investigations of the July 2026 OpenAI-Hugging Face incident motivate two questions: when an assigned task becomes impossible, does an agent stop or escalate, and can observing another agent's behavior change that decision? We study these questions using seven ImpossibleBench tasks with GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Fable 5.1, and Gemini 3.8 Flash in solo and three-agent settings. Under an explicit-boundary regime with clear authorization rules and restricted tools, no protected tests are modified, although the models differ substantially in whether they escalate, stop silently, or fail to terminate. Under a benchmark-native regime with open shell tools, protected-test changes occur more often after peer activity is introduced and in multi-agent runs. These crossings are typically not described as deliberate cheating: agents often interpret the conflicting test change as prior tampering and restore the file, thereby removing the protected requirement. Our results suggest that boundary crossing can arise from ambiguity about the state a rule is intended to protect, motivating explicit authorization boundaries, authenticated state provenance, and cross-agent monitoring.

</details>


### [56] [EvoOntology: A Self-Evolving Ontology Layer for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15779)
*Meiduo Chong,Shaolei Zhang,Ju Fan,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code: https://github.com/ruc-datalab/EvoOntology

Selection reason: EvoOntology 以 MCP 服务形式为 data agent 提供自演化本体层，含 builder agent 与带配对评测的自演化闭环，属于 agent 记忆/工具/自我改进的通用方法，可迁移到代码优化 agent 的知识层设计。

TL;DR: 提出 EvoOntology：一个自演化本体层，通过 MCP 服务器以模式层、内容层和工具层暴露本体，让数据智能体在运行时主动查询和交互；结合构建器智能体与自演化循环，在多个基准和 LLM 骨干上持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据智能体需在表格、文件和数据库等异构数据上执行自然语言指令，但存在智能体-数据鸿沟：数据在智能体外，智能体只能借助通用工具获取列名、文件路径等有限信息。现有直接探索原始数据源或注入人工语义层的方法难以扩展到大异构数据源，也难以适应不同智能体行为。

Method: EvoOntology 将本体封装为 MCP 服务器，包括模式层、内容层和工具层，使智能体可在运行时主动查询并与本体交互。系统引入构建器智能体进行自主本体构建，并设计自演化循环：通过归因引导的类型化编辑持续精炼本体，且编辑只有在经过骨干条件配对评估后才被接受。

Result: 在三个广泛采用的数据智能体基准和四个 LLM 骨干上的实验表明，EvoOntology 始终优于强基线及现有语义层方法，有效弥合智能体-数据鸿沟，并支持对异构数据更有效的交互。

Conclusion: EvoOntology 通过自演化本体层弥合智能体与异构数据之间的鸿沟，提升数据智能体在复杂异构数据环境中的交互与任务执行效果，且代码已公开。

中文摘要：数据智能体旨在对异构数据（包括表格、文件和数据库）执行自然语言指令。然而，数据智能体面临一个具有挑战性的智能体-数据鸿沟：异构数据位于智能体之外，而智能体只能通过通用工具访问它（例如列名和文件路径）。现有方法要么让智能体直接探索原始数据源，要么将人工构建的语义层注入提示中。然而，二者既难以扩展到大规模异构数据源，也无法适应不同的智能体行为。在本文中，我们提出 EvoOntology，一种面向数据智能体的自演化本体层。EvoOntology 将本体封装为一个 MCP 服务器，包含模式层、内容层和工具层，使智能体能够在运行时主动查询并与本体交互。为此，我们引入一个构建器智能体用于自主本体构建，以及一个自演化循环，通过归因引导的类型化编辑持续精炼本体，这些编辑只有在经过骨干条件配对评估后才会被接受。在三个广泛采用的数据智能体基准上、使用四个 LLM 骨干进行的实验表明，EvoOntology 始终优于强基线及现有语义层方法，有效弥合智能体-数据鸿沟，并实现对异构数据更有效的交互。代码：https://github.com/ruc-datalab/EvoOntology

Original abstract: Data agents aim to fulfill natural-language instructions over heterogeneous data, including tables, files, and databases. However, data agents face a challenging agent-data gap: heterogeneous data resides outside the agent, while the agent can access it (e.g., column names and file paths) only through generic tools. Existing approaches either let agents directly explore raw data sources or inject manually constructed semantic layers into prompts. However, neither scales well to large heterogeneous data sources nor adapts to different agent behaviors. In this paper, we introduce EvoOntology, a self-evolving ontology layer for data agents. EvoOntology encapsulates the ontology as an MCP server comprising a schema layer, a content layer, and a tool layer, enabling agents to actively query and interact with the ontology at runtime. To this end, we introduce a builder agent for autonomous ontology construction and a self-evolution loop that continuously refines the ontology through attribution-guided typed edits that are accepted only after a backbone-conditional paired evaluation. Experiments on three well-adopted data-agent benchmarks with four LLM backbones demonstrate that EvoOntology consistently outperforms strong baselines and existing semantic-layer approaches, effectively bridging the agent-data gap and enabling more effective interaction with heterogeneous data. Code: https://github.com/ruc-datalab/EvoOntology

</details>


### [57] [Navigating Sparse Evidence: Agentic Visual RAG via Explicit Context Selection and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15800)
*Yucheng Shen,Lingyong Yan,Jiulong Wu,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jianmin WU,Dawei Yin,Min Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 7 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: SCoRE 提出统一 agent 循环做显式证据选择与整合，维护文本 ledger 并配合冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知 RL，对 agent 上下文管理、轨迹复用与训练机制有明确可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出SCoRE，一个用于VRAG的统一智能体循环，在探索中显式选择与整合视觉证据，并通过冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知强化学习实现端到端优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRAG中的答案相关视觉证据稀疏且可能跨页分散；现有智能体方法依赖原始探索轨迹或压缩文本记忆，而非显式组织的支持图像集合，导致答案易受探索噪声干扰，且证据支持的推理轨迹不清晰。瓶颈不仅在证据发现，也在答案生成前的证据保存与组织。

Method: SCoRE在探索阶段维护文本账本，仅保留查询相关观察及其来源指针，从而保留早期证据并限制视觉上下文；终止时重新加载被引用的原始图像，将视觉证据整合并按逻辑顺序排列用于回答。该设计将最终推理与探索性试错解耦，并通过索引化的论断到图像链接实现严格视觉 grounding。训练上结合过滤后的冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知强化学习，奖励涵盖证据覆盖、整合紧凑性和答案正确性。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数据或指标；所述结果为方法层面的效果主张：保留关键证据、保持视觉上下文有界、解耦最终推理与探索试错、增强视觉 grounding，并支持统一 rollout 的端到端优化。

Conclusion: SCoRE通过显式证据选择与整合，针对VRAG中证据稀疏、跨页分散及证据组织不足的问题，提升答案生成的鲁棒性与可追溯性，并为该统一智能体流程提供端到端训练范式。

中文摘要：视觉检索增强生成（VRAG）通过检索相关页面图像作为视觉证据并对其内容进行推理，使模型能够导航并回答关于视觉丰富文档的查询。然而，有效利用这种视觉证据通常受到两个主要挑战的阻碍。第一，与答案相关的证据稀疏，可能集中在一页的一小部分区域，或分散在多个页面中。第二，现有的智能体方法通常基于原始探索轨迹或压缩的文本记忆生成答案，而非显式组织的支持图像集合，使答案易受探索噪声影响，并遮蔽了有证据支持的推理轨迹。我们认为，瓶颈不仅在于证据发现，还在于答案生成前的证据保存与组织。我们提出SCoRE（Selection and Consolidation for Robust Evidence，面向稳健证据的选择与整合），一个用于显式证据选择与整合的统一智能体循环。在探索过程中，SCoRE在维护的文本账本中仅保留与查询相关的观察及其来源指针，在保留早期证据的同时保持视觉上下文有界。在终止时，它重新加载所引用的原始图像，并整合视觉证据以用于回答，将其安排为逻辑序列。这将最终推理与探索性试错解耦，同时通过索引化的论断到图像链接确保严格的视觉 grounding。为实现这一统一 rollout 的端到端优化，我们的训练范式将过滤后的冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知强化学习相结合，其奖励促进证据覆盖、整合紧凑性和答案正确性。

Original abstract: Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (VRAG) empowers models to navigate and answer queries about visually rich documents by retrieving relevant page images as visual evidence and reasoning over their content. However, effectively utilizing this visual evidence is usually impeded by two main challenges. First, answer-relevant evidence is sparse and may be concentrated in a small region of one page or dispersed across multiple pages. Second, existing agentic methods often generate answers based on raw exploration trajectories or compressed textual memories rather than an explicitly organized set of supporting images, making answers susceptible to exploration noise and obscuring the evidence-backed reasoning trace. We argue that the bottleneck lies not only in evidence discovery but also in its preservation and organization before answer generation. We propose SCoRE (Selection and Consolidation for Robust Evidence), a unified agent loop for explicit evidence selection and consolidation. During exploration, SCoRE retains only query-relevant observations and their source pointers in a maintained textual ledger, preserving earlier evidence while keeping the visual context bounded. At termination, it reloads the referenced original images and consolidates the visual evidence for answering, arranging it into a logical sequence. This decouples final reasoning from exploratory trial-and-error while ensuring strict visual grounding via indexed claim-to-image linkages.
